162 | |             ) -> dict | None:

164 | |                 Calculate factor snapshot for

167 | |
168 | |                 try:

172 | |                         return None
173 | |
174 | |                     history = pd.read_parquet(history_file)
175 | |
176 | |                     history["Date"] = pd.to_datetime(history["Date"])
177 | |
178 | |                     history = history[history["Date"] <= snapshot_date]
179 | |
180 | |                     if len(history) < TRADING_DAYS:
181 | |                         return None
182 | |
183 | |                     # =========================================
184 | |                     # PRICE SERIES
185 | |                     # =========================================
186 | |
187 | |                     close = pd.to_numeric(

191 | |

193 | |                         history["High"],
194 | |                         errors="coerce",
195 | |                     )
196 | |
197 | |                     low = pd.to_numeric(
198 | |                         history["Low"],
199 | |                         errors="coerce",
200 | |                     )
201 | |
202 | |                     volume = pd.to_numeric(
203 | |                         history["Volume"],
204 | |                         errors="coerce",
205 | |                     )
206 | |
207 | |                     if len(close) < TRADING_DAYS:
208 | |                         return None
209 | |
210 | |                     returns = close.pct_change().dropna()
211 | |
212 | |                     # =========================================
213 | |                     # MOMENTUM
214 | |                     # =========================================
215 | |
216 | |                     momentum_1m = close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-21] - 1
217 | |
218 | |                     momentum_3m = close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-63] - 1
219 | |
220 | |                     momentum_6m = close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-126] - 1
221 | |
222 | |                     momentum_12m = close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-252] - 1
223 | |
224 | |                     # =========================================
225 | |                     # VOLATILITY
226 | |                     # =========================================
227 | |
228 | |                     volatility_20d = returns.tail(20).std() * np.sqrt(TRADING_DAYS)
229 | |
230 | |                     volatility_60d = returns.tail(60).std() * np.sqrt(TRADING_DAYS)
231 | |
232 | |                     # =========================================
233 | |                     # ATR
234 | |                     # =========================================
235 | |
236 | |                     previous_close = close.shift(1)
237 | |
238 | |                     true_range = pd.concat(
239 | |                         [
240 | |                             high - low,
241 | |                             (high - previous_close).abs(),
242 | |                             (low - previous_close).abs(),
243 | |                         ],
244 | |                         axis=1,
245 | |                     ).max(axis=1)
246 | |
247 | |                     atr_14 = true_range.tail(14).mean()

144 | |
145 | |         Validate

149 | |         Validate dataframe
150 | |
151 | |         Generate metadata
152 | |
153 | |         Return LoadResult

157 | |

159 | |
160 | |         self._validate_source()

162 | |         logger.info("Loading %s", self.source)
163 | |

165 | |             dataframe = self._read()
166 | |
167 | |         except Exception as exc:
168 | |             self.on_failure(exc)

171 | |

173 | |

176 | |         metadata = self._build_metadata(dataframe)
177 | |

179 | |
180 | |         self.after_load(dataframe)
181 | |
182 | |         result = self.cache(result)
183 | |
184 | |         self.audit(result)
185 | |
186 | |         logger.info("Loaded %s (%d rows)", self.source.name, len(dataframe))
187 | |
188 | |         self.on_success(result)

191 | |
192 | |     # ======================================================
193 | |     # SOURCE VALIDATION
194 | |     # ======================================================
195 | |
196 | |     def _validate_source(self) -> None:
197 | |         """
198 | |         Validate source file before loading.
199 | |         """
200 | |
201 | |         if not self.source.exists():
202 | |             raise DataLoadError(f"File does not exist: {self.source}")
203 | |
204 | |         if not self.source.is_file():
205 | |             raise DataLoadError(f"Not a valid file: {self.source}")
206 | |
207 | |         if self.source.stat().st_size == 0:
208 | |             raise EmptyDatasetError(f"File is empty: {self.source}")
209 | |
210 | |     # ======================================================
211 | |     # DATAFRAME VALIDATION
212 | |     # ======================================================
213 | |
214 | |     def _validate_dataframe(self, dataframe: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
215 | |         """
216 | |         Validate dataframe after loading.
217 | |         """
218 | |
219 | |         if dataframe is None:
220 | |             raise DataLoadError("Loader returned None.")
221 | |
222 | |         if dataframe.empty:
223 | |             raise EmptyDatasetError(f"{self.source.name} contains no records.")
224 | |
225 | |         if dataframe.columns.has_duplicates:
226 | |             raise DataLoadError("Duplicate column names detected.")
227 | |
228 | |     # ======================================================
229 | |     # REQUIRED COLUMN VALIDATION
230 | |     # ======================================================
231 | |
232 | |     def validate_columns(self: pd.DataFrame, required_columns: list[str]) -> None:
233 | |         """
234 | |         Validate required columns.
235 | |         """
236 | |
237 | |         missing = [column for column in required_columns if column not in self.columns]
238 | |
239 | |         if missing:
240 | |             raise MissingColumnError(f"Missing columns: {missing}")
241 | |
242 | |     # ======================================================
243 | |     # CHECKSUM
244 | |     # ======================================================
245 | |
246 | |     def _generate_checksum(self) -> str:
247 | |         """
248 | |         SHA256 checksum.
249 | |         """
250 | |
251 | |         with self.source.open("rb") as stream:
252 | |             return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()

256 | |     # ======================================================
257 | |
258 | |     def _build_metadata(self, dataframe: pd.DataFrame) -> LoadMetadata:
259 | |         """
260 | |         Build institutional metadata.
261 | |         """
262 | |
263 | |         elapsed = (perf_counter() - self.start_time) * 1000
264 | |
265 | |         return LoadMetadata(
266 | |             source=self.source,
267 | |             loader_name=self.__class__.__name__,
268 | |             loaded_at=datetime.now(UTC),
269 | |             elapsed_ms=round(elapsed, 2),
270 | |             file_size_bytes=self.source.stat().st_size,
271 | |             checksum=self._generate_checksum(),
272 | |             rows=len(dataframe),
273 | |             columns=len(dataframe.columns),
274 | |             column_names=list(dataframe.columns),
275 | |             memory_usage_bytes=int(dataframe.memory_usage(deep=True).sum()),
276 | |             dtypes={column: str(dtype) for column, dtype in dataframe.dtypes.items()},
277 | |         )
278 | |
279 | |     # ======================================================
280 | |     # ABSTRACT
281 | |     # ======================================================
282 | |
283 | |     @abstractmethod
284 | |     def _read(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
285 | |         """
286 | |         Concrete loaders implement this.
287 | |
288 | |         Examples
289 | |
290 | |         CSVLoader
291 | |
292 | |         ParquetLoader
293 | |
294 | |         ExcelLoader
295 | |
296 | |         DuckDBLoader
297 | |         """
298 | |
299 | |         raise NotImplementedError
300 | |
301 | |     # ======================================================
302 | |     # DATA PROFILE
303 | |     # ======================================================
304 | |
305 | |     def profile(self, dataframe: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, Any]:
306 | |         """
307 | |         Build a lightweight statistical profile
308 | |         of the loaded dataset.
309 | |
310 | |         Used by
311 | |
312 | |         • Repositories
313 | |
314 | |         • Validation
315 | |
316 | |         • Diagnostics
317 | |
318 | |         • Audit
319 | |         """
320 | |
321 | |         memory_mb = dataframe.memory_usage(deep=True).sum() / 1024 / 1024
322 | |
323 | |         duplicate_rows = int(dataframe.duplicated().sum())
324 | |
325 | |         total_nulls = int(dataframe.isna().sum().sum())
326 | |
327 | |         total_cells = dataframe.shape[0] * dataframe.shape[1]
328 | |
329 | |         null_percentage = (
330 | |             0.0 if total_cells == 0 else round(total_nulls / total_cells * 100, 2)
331 | |         )
332 | |
333 | |         return {
334 | |             "rows": len(dataframe),
335 | |             "columns": len(dataframe.columns),
336 | |             "memory_mb": round(memory_mb, 3),
337 | |             "duplicate_rows": duplicate_rows,
338 | |             "total_nulls": total_nulls,
339 | |             "null_percentage": null_percentage,
340 | |             "dtypes": {
341 | |                 column: str(dtype) for column, dtype in dataframe.dtypes.items()
342 | |             },
343 | |         }
344 | |
345 | |     # ======================================================
346 | |     # LOG DATASET
347 | |     # ======================================================
348 | |
349 | |     def log_statistics(self, dataframe: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
350 | |         """
351 | |         Log dataset statistics.
352 | |         """
353 | |
354 | |         stats = self.profile(dataframe)
355 | |
356 | |         logger.info(
357 | |             "Dataset Statistics | "
358 | |             "Rows=%d | "
359 | |             "Columns=%d | "
360 | |             "Memory=%.3f MB | "
361 | |             "Duplicates=%d | "
362 | |             "Nulls=%d (%.2f%%)",
363 | |             stats["rows"],
364 | |             stats["columns"],
365 | |             stats["memory_mb"],
366 | |             stats["duplicate_rows"],
367 | |             stats["total_nulls"],
368 | |             stats["null_percentage"],
369 | |         )
370 | |

 76 | |         """
 77 | |         Membership test by symbol.
 78 | |         """
 79 | |
 80 | |         return any(position.symbol == symbol for position in self)
 81 | |
 82 | |     def __getitem__(self, key: int | str) -> PortfolioPosition:
 83 | |         """
 84 | |         Access by index or symbol.
 85 | |         """
 86 | |
 87 | |         if isinstance(key, int):
 88 | |             return self.positions[key]
 89 | |
 90 | |         if isinstance(key, str):
 91 | |             position = self.get(key)
 92 | |
 93 | |             if position is None:
 94 | |                 raise KeyError(f"Portfolio position '{key}' not found.")
 95 | |
 96 | |             return position
 97 | |

too-many-public-methods: Too many public methods (47 > 20)
    --> orchestration/dependency_graph.py:36:1
     |
  36 | / class DependencyGraph:
  37 | |     """
  38 | |     Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG)
  39 | |     representing execution dependencies
  40 | |     between all registered engines.
  41 | |
  42 | |     Graph Direction
  43 | |
  44 | |         Dependency
  45 | |              │
  46 | |              ▼
  47 | |
  48 | |         Factor Engine
  49 | |              │
  50 | |              ▼
  51 | |
  52 | |         Signal Engine
  53 | |              │
  54 | |              ▼
  55 | |
  56 | |         Portfolio Engine
  57 | |
  58 | |     Internally stored as
  59 | |
  60 | |         Factor
  61 | |             ─────► Signal
  62 | |
  63 | |         Signal
  64 | |             ─────► Portfolio
  65 | |     """
  66 | |
  67 | |     # =====================================================
  68 | |     # CONSTRUCTOR
  69 | |     # =====================================================
  70 | |
  71 | |     def __init__(
  72 | |         self,
  73 | |         registry: EngineRegistry,
  74 | |     ) -> None:
  75 | |
  76 | |         self.registry = registry
  77 | |
  78 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
  79 | |         # Forward Graph
  80 | |         #
  81 | |         # dependency -> dependents
  82 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
  83 | |
  84 | |         self._graph: dict[
  85 | |             str,
  86 | |             set[str],
  87 | |         ] = defaultdict(set)
  88 | |
  89 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
  90 | |         # Reverse Graph
  91 | |         #
  92 | |         # engine -> dependencies
  93 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
  94 | |
  95 | |         self._reverse_graph: dict[
  96 | |             str,
  97 | |             set[str],
  98 | |         ] = defaultdict(set)
  99 | |
 100 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 101 | |         # Engine Registry Snapshot
 102 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 103 | |
 104 | |         self._nodes: dict[
 105 | |             str,
 106 | |             type[BaseEngine],
 107 | |         ] = {}
 108 | |
 109 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 110 | |         # In-degree Cache
 111 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 112 | |
 113 | |         self._indegree: dict[
 114 | |             str,
 115 | |             int,
 116 | |         ] = defaultdict(int)
 117 | |
 118 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 119 | |         # Build Status
 120 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 121 | |
 122 | |         self._built = False
 123 | |
 124 | |     # =====================================================
 125 | |     # PROPERTIES
 126 | |     # =====================================================
 127 | |
 128 | |     @property
 129 | |     def node_count(
 130 | |         self,
 131 | |     ) -> int:
 132 | |
 133 | |         return len(self._nodes)
 134 | |
 135 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 136 | |
 137 | |     @property
 138 | |     def edge_count(
 139 | |         self,
 140 | |     ) -> int:
 141 | |
 142 | |         return sum(len(edges) for edges in self._graph.values())
 143 | |
 144 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 145 | |
 146 | |     @property
 147 | |     def is_built(
 148 | |         self,
 149 | |     ) -> bool:
 150 | |
 151 | |         return self._built
 152 | |
 153 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 154 | |
 155 | |     @property
 156 | |     def nodes(
 157 | |         self,
 158 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 159 | |
 160 | |         return sorted(self._nodes.keys())
 161 | |
 162 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 163 | |
 164 | |     @property
 165 | |     def roots(
 166 | |         self,
 167 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 168 | |
 169 | |         return sorted(
 170 | |             [
 171 | |                 node
 172 | |                 for (
 173 | |                     node,
 174 | |                     degree,
 175 | |                 ) in self._indegree.items()
 176 | |                 if degree == 0
 177 | |             ],
 178 | |             key=lambda node: (
 179 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 180 | |                 node,
 181 | |             ),
 182 | |         )
 183 | |
 184 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 185 | |
 186 | |     @property
 187 | |     def leaves(
 188 | |         self,
 189 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 190 | |
 191 | |         return sorted(
 192 | |             [node for node in self._nodes if not self._graph[node]],
 193 | |             key=lambda node: (
 194 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 195 | |                 node,
 196 | |             ),
 197 | |         )
 198 | |
 199 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 200 | |
 201 | |     @property
 202 | |     def isolated_nodes(
 203 | |         self,
 204 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 205 | |
 206 | |         isolated = []
 207 | |
 208 | |         for node in self._nodes:
 209 | |             if not self._graph[node] and not self._reverse_graph[node]:
 210 | |                 isolated.append(node)
 211 | |
 212 | |         return sorted(isolated)
 213 | |
 214 | |     # =====================================================
 215 | |     # BUILD GRAPH
 216 | |     # =====================================================
 217 | |
 218 | |     def build(self) -> None:
 219 | |         """
 220 | |         Build the dependency graph from the
 221 | |         registered engines.
 222 | |         """
 223 | |
 224 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 225 | |         # Reset current graph
 226 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 227 | |
 228 | |         self._graph.clear()
 229 | |
 230 | |         self._reverse_graph.clear()
 231 | |
 232 | |         self._nodes.clear()
 233 | |
 234 | |         self._indegree.clear()
 235 | |
 236 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 237 | |         # Register every engine
 238 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 239 | |
 240 | |         for engine in self.registry.sorted_by_priority():
 241 | |             self.add_node(engine)
 242 | |
 243 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 244 | |         # Register dependencies
 245 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 246 | |
 247 | |         for engine in self.registry.sorted_by_priority():
 248 | |             for dependency in engine.DEPENDS_ON:
 249 | |                 self.add_dependency(
 250 | |                     dependency,
 251 | |                     engine.NAME,
 252 | |                 )
 253 | |
 254 | |         self._built = True
 255 | |
 256 | |     # =====================================================
 257 | |     # REBUILD GRAPH
 258 | |     # =====================================================
 259 | |
 260 | |     def rebuild(self) -> None:
 261 | |         """
 262 | |         Rebuild graph from registry.
 263 | |         """
 264 | |
 265 | |         self.build()
 266 | |
 267 | |     # =====================================================
 268 | |     # ADD NODE
 269 | |     # =====================================================
 270 | |
 271 | |     def add_node(
 272 | |         self,
 273 | |         engine: type[BaseEngine],
 274 | |     ) -> None:
 275 | |         """
 276 | |         Register an engine as a graph node.
 277 | |         """
 278 | |
 279 | |         if engine.NAME in self._nodes:
 280 | |             return
 281 | |
 282 | |         self._nodes[engine.NAME] = engine
 283 | |
 284 | |         self._graph.setdefault(
 285 | |             engine.NAME,
 286 | |             set(),
 287 | |         )
 288 | |
 289 | |         self._reverse_graph.setdefault(
 290 | |             engine.NAME,
 291 | |             set(),
 292 | |         )
 293 | |
 294 | |         self._indegree.setdefault(
 295 | |             engine.NAME,
 296 | |             0,
 297 | |         )
 298 | |
 299 | |     # =====================================================
 300 | |     # REMOVE NODE
 301 | |     # =====================================================
 302 | |
 303 | |     def remove_node(
 304 | |         self,
 305 | |         engine_name: str,
 306 | |     ) -> None:
 307 | |         """
 308 | |         Remove an engine from the graph.
 309 | |         """
 310 | |
 311 | |         if engine_name not in self._nodes:
 312 | |             return
 313 | |
 314 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 315 | |         # Remove outgoing edges
 316 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 317 | |
 318 | |         for child in list(self._graph[engine_name]):
 319 | |             self.remove_dependency(
 320 | |                 engine_name,
 321 | |                 child,
 322 | |             )
 323 | |
 324 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 325 | |         # Remove incoming edges
 326 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 327 | |
 328 | |         for parent in list(self._reverse_graph[engine_name]):
 329 | |             self.remove_dependency(
 330 | |                 parent,
 331 | |                 engine_name,
 332 | |             )
 333 | |
 334 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 335 | |         # Remove node
 336 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
 337 | |
 338 | |         self._graph.pop(
 339 | |             engine_name,
 340 | |             None,
 341 | |         )
 342 | |
 343 | |         self._reverse_graph.pop(
 344 | |             engine_name,
 345 | |             None,
 346 | |         )
 347 | |
 348 | |         self._indegree.pop(
 349 | |             engine_name,
 350 | |             None,
 351 | |         )
 352 | |
 353 | |         self._nodes.pop(
 354 | |             engine_name,
 355 | |             None,
 356 | |         )
 357 | |
 358 | |     # =====================================================
 359 | |     # ADD DEPENDENCY
 360 | |     # =====================================================
 361 | |
 362 | |     def add_dependency(
 363 | |         self,
 364 | |         dependency: str,
 365 | |         engine: str,
 366 | |     ) -> None:
 367 | |         """
 368 | |         Register a dependency.
 369 | |
 370 | |         dependency ---> engine
 371 | |         """
 372 | |
 373 | |         if dependency not in self._nodes:
 374 | |             raise KeyError(f"Unknown dependency '{dependency}'.")
 375 | |
 376 | |         if engine not in self._nodes:
 377 | |             raise KeyError(f"Unknown engine '{engine}'.")
 378 | |
 379 | |         if engine in self._graph[dependency]:
 380 | |             return
 381 | |
 382 | |         self._graph[dependency].add(engine)
 383 | |
 384 | |         self._reverse_graph[engine].add(dependency)
 385 | |
 386 | |         self._indegree[engine] += 1
 387 | |
 388 | |     # =====================================================
 389 | |     # REMOVE DEPENDENCY
 390 | |     # =====================================================
 391 | |
 392 | |     def remove_dependency(
 393 | |         self,
 394 | |         dependency: str,
 395 | |         engine: str,
 396 | |     ) -> None:
 397 | |         """
 398 | |         Remove a dependency edge.
 399 | |         """
 400 | |
 401 | |         if dependency not in self._graph:
 402 | |             return
 403 | |
 404 | |         if engine not in self._graph[dependency]:
 405 | |             return
 406 | |
 407 | |         self._graph[dependency].remove(engine)
 408 | |
 409 | |         self._reverse_graph[engine].remove(dependency)
 410 | |
 411 | |         self._indegree[engine] -= 1
 412 | |
 413 | |     # =====================================================
 414 | |     # GRAPH RESET
 415 | |     # =====================================================
 416 | |
 417 | |     def clear(self) -> None:
 418 | |         """
 419 | |         Remove every node and edge.
 420 | |         """
 421 | |
 422 | |         self._graph.clear()
 423 | |
 424 | |         self._reverse_graph.clear()
 425 | |
 426 | |         self._nodes.clear()
 427 | |
 428 | |         self._indegree.clear()
 429 | |
 430 | |         self._built = False
 431 | |
 432 | |     # =====================================================
 433 | |     # NODE QUERIES
 434 | |     # =====================================================
 435 | |
 436 | |     def has_node(
 437 | |         self,
 438 | |         engine_name: str,
 439 | |     ) -> bool:
 440 | |         """
 441 | |         Check whether a node exists.
 442 | |         """
 443 | |
 444 | |         return engine_name in self._nodes
 445 | |
 446 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 447 | |
 448 | |     def has_dependency(
 449 | |         self,
 450 | |         dependency: str,
 451 | |         engine: str,
 452 | |     ) -> bool:
 453 | |         """
 454 | |         Return True if
 455 | |
 456 | |             dependency ---> engine
 457 | |
 458 | |         exists.
 459 | |         """
 460 | |
 461 | |         return dependency in self._graph and engine in self._graph[dependency]
 462 | |
 463 | |     # =====================================================
 464 | |     # DIRECT RELATIONSHIPS
 465 | |     # =====================================================
 466 | |
 467 | |     def upstream(
 468 | |         self,
 469 | |         engine_name: str,
 470 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 471 | |         """
 472 | |         Immediate dependencies.
 473 | |
 474 | |         engine
 475 | |             ↑
 476 | |         dependencies
 477 | |         """
 478 | |
 479 | |         if not self.has_node(engine_name):
 480 | |             raise KeyError(f"Unknown engine '{engine_name}'.")
 481 | |
 482 | |         return sorted(
 483 | |             self._reverse_graph[engine_name],
 484 | |             key=lambda node: (
 485 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 486 | |                 node,
 487 | |             ),
 488 | |         )
 489 | |
 490 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 491 | |
 492 | |     def downstream(
 493 | |         self,
 494 | |         engine_name: str,
 495 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 496 | |         """
 497 | |         Immediate dependents.
 498 | |
 499 | |         engine
 500 | |             ↓
 501 | |         dependents
 502 | |         """
 503 | |
 504 | |         if not self.has_node(engine_name):
 505 | |             raise KeyError(f"Unknown engine '{engine_name}'.")
 506 | |
 507 | |         return sorted(
 508 | |             self._graph[engine_name],
 509 | |             key=lambda node: (
 510 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 511 | |                 node,
 512 | |             ),
 513 | |         )
 514 | |
 515 | |     # =====================================================
 516 | |     # RECURSIVE RELATIONSHIPS
 517 | |     # =====================================================
 518 | |
 519 | |     def ancestors(
 520 | |         self,
 521 | |         engine_name: str,
 522 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 523 | |         """
 524 | |         Return every upstream dependency.
 525 | |         """
 526 | |
 527 | |         if not self.has_node(engine_name):
 528 | |             raise KeyError(engine_name)
 529 | |
 530 | |         visited: set[str] = set()
 531 | |
 532 | |         def dfs(
 533 | |             node: str,
 534 | |         ) -> None:
 535 | |
 536 | |             for parent in self._reverse_graph[node]:
 537 | |                 if parent in visited:
 538 | |                     continue
 539 | |
 540 | |                 visited.add(parent)
 541 | |
 542 | |                 dfs(parent)
 543 | |
 544 | |         dfs(engine_name)
 545 | |
 546 | |         return sorted(
 547 | |             visited,
 548 | |             key=lambda node: (
 549 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 550 | |                 node,
 551 | |             ),
 552 | |         )
 553 | |
 554 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 555 | |
 556 | |     def descendants(
 557 | |         self,
 558 | |         engine_name: str,
 559 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 560 | |         """
 561 | |         Return every downstream engine.
 562 | |         """
 563 | |
 564 | |         if not self.has_node(engine_name):
 565 | |             raise KeyError(engine_name)
 566 | |
 567 | |         visited: set[str] = set()
 568 | |
 569 | |         def dfs(
 570 | |             node: str,
 571 | |         ) -> None:
 572 | |
 573 | |             for child in self._graph[node]:
 574 | |                 if child in visited:
 575 | |                     continue
 576 | |
 577 | |                 visited.add(child)
 578 | |
 579 | |                 dfs(child)
 580 | |
 581 | |         dfs(engine_name)
 582 | |
 583 | |         return sorted(
 584 | |             visited,
 585 | |             key=lambda node: (
 586 | |                 self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 587 | |                 node,
 588 | |             ),
 589 | |         )
 590 | |
 591 | |     # =====================================================
 592 | |     # ALIASES
 593 | |     # =====================================================
 594 | |
 595 | |     def dependencies(
 596 | |         self,
 597 | |         engine_name: str,
 598 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 599 | |         """
 600 | |         Alias for upstream().
 601 | |         """
 602 | |
 603 | |         return self.upstream(engine_name)
 604 | |
 605 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 606 | |
 607 | |     def dependents(
 608 | |         self,
 609 | |         engine_name: str,
 610 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 611 | |         """
 612 | |         Alias for downstream().
 613 | |         """
 614 | |
 615 | |         return self.downstream(engine_name)
 616 | |
 617 | |     # =====================================================
 618 | |     # GRAPH INSPECTION
 619 | |     # =====================================================
 620 | |
 621 | |     def neighbors(
 622 | |         self,
 623 | |         engine_name: str,
 624 | |     ) -> dict[
 625 | |         str,
 626 | |         list[str],
 627 | |     ]:
 628 | |         """
 629 | |         Return adjacent nodes.
 630 | |         """
 631 | |
 632 | |         return {
 633 | |             "upstream": self.upstream(engine_name),
 634 | |             "downstream": self.downstream(engine_name),
 635 | |         }
 636 | |
 637 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 638 | |
 639 | |     def degree(
 640 | |         self,
 641 | |         engine_name: str,
 642 | |     ) -> dict[str, int]:
 643 | |         """
 644 | |         Return node degree.
 645 | |         """
 646 | |
 647 | |         return {
 648 | |             "indegree": len(self._reverse_graph[engine_name]),
 649 | |             "outdegree": len(self._graph[engine_name]),
 650 | |             "total": len(self._reverse_graph[engine_name])
 651 | |             + len(self._graph[engine_name]),
 652 | |         }
 653 | |
 654 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 655 | |
 656 | |     def adjacency_list(
 657 | |         self,
 658 | |     ) -> dict[
 659 | |         str,
 660 | |         list[str],
 661 | |     ]:
 662 | |         """
 663 | |         Return graph as an
 664 | |         adjacency list.
 665 | |         """
 666 | |
 667 | |         return {
 668 | |             node: sorted(
 669 | |                 children,
 670 | |                 key=lambda n: (
 671 | |                     self._nodes[n].PRIORITY,
 672 | |                     n,
 673 | |                 ),
 674 | |             )
 675 | |             for (
 676 | |                 node,
 677 | |                 children,
 678 | |             ) in self._graph.items()
 679 | |         }
 680 | |
 681 | |     # =====================================================
 682 | |     # VALIDATION
 683 | |     # =====================================================
 684 | |
 685 | |     def validate_dependencies(
 686 | |         self,
 687 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 688 | |         """
 689 | |         Validate that every declared dependency
 690 | |         exists inside the graph.
 691 | |         """
 692 | |
 693 | |         errors: list[str] = []
 694 | |
 695 | |         for engine in self._nodes.values():
 696 | |             for dependency in engine.DEPENDS_ON:
 697 | |                 if dependency not in self._nodes:
 698 | |                     errors.append(
 699 | |                         f"{engine.NAME} "
 700 | |                         f"depends on "
 701 | |                         f"'{dependency}' "
 702 | |                         f"which is not "
 703 | |                         f"registered."
 704 | |                     )
 705 | |
 706 | |         return errors
 707 | |
 708 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 709 | |
 710 | |     def validate_nodes(
 711 | |         self,
 712 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 713 | |         """
 714 | |         Validate node integrity.
 715 | |         """
 716 | |
 717 | |         errors: list[str] = []
 718 | |
 719 | |         for node in self._nodes:
 720 | |             if node not in self._graph:
 721 | |                 errors.append(f"Missing graph node '{node}'.")
 722 | |
 723 | |             if node not in self._reverse_graph:
 724 | |                 errors.append(f"Missing reverse graph node '{node}'.")
 725 | |
 726 | |         return errors
 727 | |
 728 | |     # =====================================================
 729 | |     # CYCLE DETECTION
 730 | |     # =====================================================
 731 | |
 732 | |     def detect_cycles(
 733 | |         self,
 734 | |     ) -> list[list[str]]:
 735 | |         """
 736 | |         Detect dependency cycles.
 737 | |
 738 | |         Returns a list of cycles.
 739 | |         """
 740 | |
 741 | |         visited: set[str] = set()
 742 | |
 743 | |         stack: set[str] = set()
 744 | |
 745 | |         cycles: list[list[str]] = []
 746 | |
 747 | |         path: list[str] = []
 748 | |
 749 | |         def dfs(node: str) -> None:
 750 | |
 751 | |             visited.add(node)
 752 | |
 753 | |             stack.add(node)
 754 | |
 755 | |             path.append(node)
 756 | |
 757 | |             for child in self._graph[node]:
 758 | |                 if child not in visited:
 759 | |                     dfs(child)
 760 | |
 761 | |                 elif child in stack:
 762 | |                     try:
 763 | |                         start = path.index(child)
 764 | |
 765 | |                         cycles.append([*path[start:], child])
 766 | |
 767 | |                     except ValueError:
 768 | |                         pass
 769 | |
 770 | |             stack.remove(node)
 771 | |
 772 | |             path.pop()
 773 | |
 774 | |         for node in self._nodes:
 775 | |             if node not in visited:
 776 | |                 dfs(node)
 777 | |
 778 | |         return cycles
 779 | |
 780 | |     # =====================================================
 781 | |     # TOPOLOGICAL SORT
 782 | |     # =====================================================
 783 | |
 784 | |     def execution_order(
 785 | |         self,
 786 | |     ) -> list[str]:
 787 | |         """
 788 | |         Compute dependency order using
 789 | |         Kahn's Algorithm.
 790 | |         """
 791 | |
 792 | |         indegree = dict(self._indegree)
 793 | |
 794 | |         ready = deque(
 795 | |             sorted(
 796 | |                 [node for node, degree in indegree.items() if degree == 0],
 797 | |                 key=lambda node: (
 798 | |                     self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 799 | |                     node,
 800 | |                 ),
 801 | |             )
 802 | |         )
 803 | |
 804 | |         order: list[str] = []
 805 | |
 806 | |         while ready:
 807 | |             node = ready.popleft()
 808 | |
 809 | |             order.append(node)
 810 | |
 811 | |             children = sorted(
 812 | |                 self._graph[node],
 813 | |                 key=lambda child: (
 814 | |                     self._nodes[child].PRIORITY,
 815 | |                     child,
 816 | |                 ),
 817 | |             )
 818 | |
 819 | |             for child in children:
 820 | |                 indegree[child] -= 1
 821 | |
 822 | |                 if indegree[child] == 0:
 823 | |                     ready.append(child)
 824 | |
 825 | |         if len(order) != self.node_count:
 826 | |             raise RuntimeError("Dependency graph contains one or more cycles.")
 827 | |
 828 | |         return order
 829 | |
 830 | |     # =====================================================
 831 | |     # VALIDATE
 832 | |     # =====================================================
 833 | |
 834 | |     def validate(
 835 | |         self,
 836 | |     ) -> dict[str, object]:
 837 | |         """
 838 | |         Validate the complete graph.
 839 | |         """
 840 | |
 841 | |         dependency_errors = self.validate_dependencies()
 842 | |
 843 | |         node_errors = self.validate_nodes()
 844 | |
 845 | |         cycles = self.detect_cycles()
 846 | |
 847 | |         return {
 848 | |             "valid": (not dependency_errors and not node_errors and not cycles),
 849 | |             "dependency_errors": dependency_errors,
 850 | |             "node_errors": node_errors,
 851 | |             "cycles": cycles,
 852 | |         }
 853 | |
 854 | |     # =====================================================
 855 | |     # EXECUTION LEVELS
 856 | |     # =====================================================
 857 | |
 858 | |     def execution_levels(
 859 | |         self,
 860 | |     ) -> list[list[str]]:
 861 | |         """
 862 | |         Compute execution levels.
 863 | |
 864 | |         Engines within the same level have
 865 | |         no dependency on each other and may
 866 | |         execute in parallel.
 867 | |         """
 868 | |
 869 | |         indegree = dict(self._indegree)
 870 | |
 871 | |         remaining = set(self._nodes.keys())
 872 | |
 873 | |         levels: list[list[str]] = []
 874 | |
 875 | |         while remaining:
 876 | |             ready = sorted(
 877 | |                 [node for node in remaining if indegree[node] == 0],
 878 | |                 key=lambda node: (
 879 | |                     self._nodes[node].PRIORITY,
 880 | |                     node,
 881 | |                 ),
 882 | |             )
 883 | |
 884 | |             if not ready:
 885 | |                 raise RuntimeError("Dependency cycle detected.")
 886 | |
 887 | |             levels.append(ready)
 888 | |
 889 | |             for node in ready:
 890 | |                 remaining.remove(node)
 891 | |
 892 | |                 for child in self._graph[node]:
 893 | |                     indegree[child] -= 1
 894 | |
 895 | |         return levels
 896 | |
 897 | |     # =====================================================
 898 | |     # EXECUTION PLAN
 899 | |     # =====================================================
 900 | |
 901 | |     def execution_plan(
 902 | |         self,
 903 | |     ) -> list[dict]:
 904 | |         """
 905 | |         Build execution plan suitable
 906 | |         for the Pipeline Builder.
 907 | |         """
 908 | |
 909 | |         plan = []
 910 | |
 911 | |         for level, engines in enumerate(
 912 | |             self.execution_levels(),
 913 | |             start=1,
 914 | |         ):
 915 | |             plan.append(
 916 | |                 {
 917 | |                     "level": level,
 918 | |                     "parallel": True,
 919 | |                     "engine_count": len(engines),
 920 | |                     "engines": engines,
 921 | |                 }
 922 | |             )
 923 | |
 924 | |         return plan
 925 | |
 926 | |     # =====================================================
 927 | |     # GRAPH METRICS
 928 | |     # =====================================================
 929 | |
 930 | |     def maximum_depth(
 931 | |         self,
 932 | |     ) -> int:
 933 | |         """
 934 | |         Maximum dependency depth.
 935 | |         """
 936 | |
 937 | |         return len(self.execution_levels())
 938 | |
 939 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 940 | |
 941 | |     def maximum_parallelism(
 942 | |         self,
 943 | |     ) -> int:
 944 | |         """
 945 | |         Largest number of engines that
 946 | |         may execute concurrently.
 947 | |         """
 948 | |
 949 | |         return max(
 950 | |             (len(level) for level in self.execution_levels()),
 951 | |             default=0,
 952 | |         )
 953 | |
 954 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 955 | |
 956 | |     def edge_density(
 957 | |         self,
 958 | |     ) -> float:
 959 | |         """
 960 | |         Directed graph density.
 961 | |         """
 962 | |
 963 | |         n = self.node_count
 964 | |
 965 | |         if n <= 1:
 966 | |             return 0.0
 967 | |
 968 | |         maximum_edges = n * (n - 1)
 969 | |
 970 | |         return round(
 971 | |             self.edge_count / maximum_edges,
 972 | |             4,
 973 | |         )
 974 | |
 975 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 976 | |
 977 | |     def graph_metrics(
 978 | |         self,
 979 | |     ) -> dict[str, object]:
 980 | |         """
 981 | |         Return graph statistics.
 982 | |         """
 983 | |
 984 | |         return {
 985 | |             "nodes": self.node_count,
 986 | |             "edges": self.edge_count,
 987 | |             "roots": len(self.roots),
 988 | |             "leaves": len(self.leaves),
 989 | |             "isolated": len(self.isolated_nodes),
 990 | |             "depth": self.maximum_depth(),
 991 | |             "parallelism": self.maximum_parallelism(),
 992 | |             "density": self.edge_density(),
 993 | |         }
 994 | |
 995 | |     # =====================================================
 996 | |     # CRITICAL PATH
 997 | |     # =====================================================
 998 | |
 999 | |     def critical_path(
1000 | |         self,
1001 | |     ) -> list[str]:
1002 | |         """
1003 | |         Approximate critical execution path.
1004 | |
1005 | |         Currently returns the longest
1006 | |         dependency chain based on execution
1007 | |         levels.
1008 | |         """
1009 | |
1010 | |         levels = self.execution_levels()
1011 | |
1012 | |         if not levels:
1013 | |             return []
1014 | |
1015 | |         path = []
1016 | |
1017 | |         for level in levels:
1018 | |             path.append(level[0])
1019 | |
1020 | |         return path
1021 | |
1022 | |     # =====================================================
1023 | |     # EXPORT
1024 | |     # =====================================================
1025 | |
1026 | |     def to_dict(
1027 | |         self,
1028 | |     ) -> dict[str, object]:
1029 | |         """
1030 | |         Export the graph as a serializable
1031 | |         dictionary.
1032 | |         """
1033 | |
1034 | |         return {
1035 | |             "nodes": sorted(self._nodes.keys()),
1036 | |             "edges": {
1037 | |                 node: sorted(
1038 | |                     children,
1039 | |                     key=lambda n: (
1040 | |                         self._nodes[n].PRIORITY,
1041 | |                         n,
1042 | |                     ),
1043 | |                 )
1044 | |                 for (
1045 | |                     node,
1046 | |                     children,
1047 | |                 ) in self._graph.items()
1048 | |             },
1049 | |             "metrics": self.graph_metrics(),
1050 | |             "execution_plan": self.execution_plan(),
1051 | |         }
1052 | |
1053 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
1054 | |
1055 | |     def to_json(
1056 | |         self,
1057 | |         indent: int = 4,
1058 | |     ) -> str:
1059 | |         """
1060 | |         Export graph as JSON.
1061 | |         """
1062 | |
1063 | |         return json.dumps(
1064 | |             self.to_dict(),
1065 | |             indent=indent,
1066 | |         )
1067 | |
1068 | |     # =====================================================
1069 | |     # MERMAID EXPORT

1072 | |     def to_mermaid(
1073 | |         self,
1074 | |     ) -> str:
1075 | |         """
1076 | |         Export graph in Mermaid format.
1077 | |         """
1078 | |
1079 | |         lines = [
1080 | |             "graph TD",
1081 | |         ]
1082 | |
1083 | |         for (
1084 | |             parent,
1085 | |             children,
1086 | |         ) in sorted(self._graph.items()):
1087 | |             if not children:
1088 | |                 lines.append(f"    {parent}")
1089 | |
1090 | |                 continue
1091 | |
1092 | |             for child in sorted(children):
1093 | |                 lines.append(f"    {parent} --> {child}")
1094 | |
1095 | |         return "\n".join(lines)

 43 | |     Central registry for every engine available
 44 | |     in the platform.
 45 | |
 46 | |     Engines may be
 47 | |
 48 | |     • Registered manually
 49 | |

140 | |     def register(
141 | |         self,
142 | |         engine: type[BaseEngine],
143 | |     ) -> None:
144 | |         """
145 | |         Register an engine class.
146 | |         """
147 | |
148 | |         if not issubclass(
149 | |             engine,
150 | |             BaseEngine,
151 | |         ):
152 | |             raise TypeError(f"{engine} is not a BaseEngine.")
153 | |
154 | |         if engine is BaseEngine:
155 | |             return
156 | |
157 | |         if not engine.ENABLED:
158 | |             logger.info(
159 | |                 "Skipping disabled engine: %s",
160 | |                 engine.NAME,
161 | |             )
162 | |
163 | |             return
164 | |
165 | |         if engine.NAME in self._engines:
166 | |             raise ValueError(f"Duplicate engine '{engine.NAME}'.")
167 | |
168 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
169 | |         # Register
170 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
171 | |
172 | |         self._engines[engine.NAME] = engine
173 | |
174 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
175 | |         # Build indexes
176 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
177 | |
178 | |         self._categories[engine.CATEGORY].append(engine)
179 | |
180 | |         self._stages[engine.STAGE].append(engine)
181 | |
182 | |         for tag in engine.TAGS:
183 | |             self._tags[tag].append(engine)
184 | |
185 | |         logger.info(
186 | |             "Registered engine: %s",
187 | |             engine.NAME,
188 | |         )
189 | |
190 | |     # =====================================================
191 | |     # UNREGISTER
192 | |     # =====================================================
193 | |
194 | |     def unregister(
195 | |         self,
196 | |         engine_name: str,
197 | |     ) -> None:
198 | |         """
199 | |         Remove an engine from the registry.
200 | |         """
201 | |
202 | |         if engine_name not in self._engines:
203 | |             raise KeyError(f"Unknown engine '{engine_name}'.")
204 | |
205 | |         engine = self._engines.pop(engine_name)
206 | |
207 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
208 | |         # Remove from category index
209 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
210 | |
211 | |         if engine.CATEGORY in self._categories:
212 | |             self._categories[engine.CATEGORY] = [
213 | |                 cls
214 | |                 for cls in self._categories[engine.CATEGORY]
215 | |                 if engine_name != cls.NAME
216 | |             ]
217 | |
218 | |             if not self._categories[engine.CATEGORY]:
219 | |                 del self._categories[engine.CATEGORY]
220 | |
221 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
222 | |         # Remove from stage index
223 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
224 | |
225 | |         if engine.STAGE in self._stages:
226 | |             self._stages[engine.STAGE] = [
227 | |                 cls for cls in self._stages[engine.STAGE] if engine_name != cls.NAME
228 | |             ]
229 | |
230 | |             if not self._stages[engine.STAGE]:
231 | |                 del self._stages[engine.STAGE]
232 | |
233 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
234 | |         # Remove tag index
235 | |         # ---------------------------------------------
236 | |
237 | |         for tag in engine.TAGS:
238 | |             if tag not in self._tags:
239 | |                 continue
240 | |
241 | |             self._tags[tag] = [
242 | |                 cls for cls in self._tags[tag] if engine_name != cls.NAME
243 | |             ]
244 | |
245 | |             if not self._tags[tag]:
246 | |                 del self._tags[tag]
247 | |
248 | |         logger.info(
249 | |             "Unregistered engine: %s",
250 | |             engine_name,
251 | |         )

256 | |
257 | |     def discover(
258 | |         self,
259 | |         packages: Iterable[str] | None = None,
260 | |     ) -> None:
261 | |         """
262 | |         Discover all BaseEngine implementations.
263 | |         """
264 | |
265 | |         packages = list(packages or self.DEFAULT_PACKAGES)
266 | |
267 | |         logger.info("Starting engine discovery.")
268 | |
269 | |         for package in packages:
270 | |             self._discover_package(package)
271 | |
272 | |         self._discovery_complete = True
273 | |
274 | |         logger.info("Engine discovery complete.")
275 | |
276 | |         logger.info(
277 | |             "Packages Scanned : %s",
278 | |             len(self._packages_scanned),
279 | |         )
280 | |
281 | |         logger.info(
282 | |             "Modules Scanned : %s",
283 | |             len(self._modules_scanned),
284 | |         )
285 | |
286 | |         logger.info(
287 | |             "Registered Engines : %s",
288 | |             self.engine_count,
289 | |         )

294 | |
295 | |     def _discover_package(
296 | |         self,
297 | |         package_name: str,
298 | |     ) -> None:
299 | |         """
300 | |         Discover all engines inside
301 | |         a Python package.
302 | |         """
303 | |
304 | |         try:
305 | |             package = importlib.import_module(package_name)
306 | |
307 | |         except Exception as exc:
308 | |             logger.warning(
309 | |                 "Unable to import %s (%s)",
310 | |                 package_name,
311 | |                 exc,
312 | |             )
313 | |
314 | |             return
315 | |
316 | |         self._packages_scanned.append(package_name)
317 | |
318 | |         if not hasattr(
319 | |             package,
320 | |             "__path__",
321 | |         ):
322 | |             return
323 | |
324 | |         for (
325 | |             _,
326 | |             module_name,
327 | |             _,
328 | |         ) in pkgutil.walk_packages(
329 | |             package.__path__,
330 | |             package.__name__ + ".",
331 | |         ):
332 | |             self._discover_module(module_name)
333 | |
334 | |     # =====================================================
335 | |     # MODULE DISCOVERY

337 | |
338 | |     def _discover_module(
339 | |         self,
340 | |         module_name: str,
341 | |     ) -> None:
342 | |         """
343 | |         Import and inspect
344 | |         a module.
345 | |         """
346 | |
347 | |         try:
348 | |             module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
349 | |
350 | |         except Exception as exc:
351 | |             logger.warning(
352 | |                 "Skipping module %s (%s)",
353 | |                 module_name,
354 | |                 exc,
355 | |             )
356 | |
357 | |             return
358 | |
359 | |         self._modules_scanned.append(module_name)
360 | |

362 | |

364 | |     # MODULE REGISTRATION
365 | |     # =====================================================
366 | |
367 | |     def _register_module(
368 | |         self,
369 | |         module,
370 | |     ) -> None:
371 | |         """
372 | |         Register every BaseEngine
373 | |         found inside a module.
374 | |         """
375 | |
376 | |         for (
377 | |             _,
378 | |             obj,

382 | |         ):
383 | |             if obj is BaseEngine:

385 | |

389 | |             ):
390 | |                 continue
391 | |
392 | |             self.register(obj)

705 | |     def validate_dependencies(
706 | |         self,
707 | |     ) -> list[str]:
708 | |         """
709 | |         Validate dependency graph.
710 | |
711 | |         Returns list of errors.
712 | |         """
713 | |
714 | |         errors: list[str] = []
715 | |
716 | |         for engine in self.classes():
717 | |             for dependency in engine.DEPENDS_ON:
718 | |                 if dependency not in self._engines:
719 | |                     errors.append(
720 | |                         f"{engine.NAME} "
721 | |                         f"depends on "
722 | |                         f"'{dependency}' "
723 | |                         f"which is not "
724 | |                         f"registered."
725 | |                     )
726 | |
727 | |         return errors

729 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
730 | |
731 | |     def validate_outputs(
732 | |         self,
733 | |     ) -> list[str]:
734 | |         """
735 | |         Detect duplicate output files.
736 | |         """
737 | |
738 | |         errors: list[str] = []
739 | |
740 | |         seen = {}
741 | |
742 | |         for engine in self.classes():
743 | |             for output in engine.OUTPUTS:
744 | |                 if output in seen:
745 | |                     errors.append(
746 | |                         f"Duplicate output "
747 | |                         f"'{output}' "
748 | |                         f"generated by "
749 | |                         f"{engine.NAME} "
750 | |                         f"and "
751 | |                         f"{seen[output]}."
752 | |                     )
753 | |
754 | |                 else:
755 | |                     seen[output] = engine.NAME
756 | |
757 | |         return errors
758 | |

760 | |
761 | |     def validate(
762 | |         self,
763 | |     ) -> list[str]:
764 | |         """
765 | |         Run every registry validation.
766 | |         """
767 | |
768 | |         errors = []
769 | |
770 | |         errors.extend(self.validate_dependencies())
771 | |
772 | |         errors.extend(self.validate_outputs())
773 | |
774 | |         return errors

 510 | |                 for container in (

 512 | |                     self.outputs,
 513 | |                     self.variables,

 515 | |                 )

 533 | |
 534 | |             self.outputs.pop(
 535 | |                 key,
 536 | |                 None,
 537 | |             )
 538 | |
 539 | |             self.variables.pop(
 540 | |                 key,
 541 | |                 None,
 542 | |             )
 543 | |
 544 | |             self.cache.pop(
 545 | |                 key,
 546 | |                 None,
 547 | |             )
 548 | |
 549 | |     # =====================================================
 550 | |     # SNAPSHOT
 551 | |     # =====================================================
 552 | |
 553 | |     def snapshot(
 554 | |         self,
 555 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 556 | |         """
 557 | |         Create an immutable snapshot of the current
 558 | |         execution context.
 559 | |
 560 | |         Returns
 561 | |         -------
 562 | |         Dict[str, Any]
 563 | |         """
 564 | |
 565 | |         with self._lock:
 566 | |             return {
 567 | |                 "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
 568 | |                 "outputs": dict(self.outputs),
 569 | |                 "variables": dict(self.variables),
 570 | |                 "cache": dict(self.cache),
 571 | |                 "artifacts": sorted(self.artifacts),
 572 | |                 "files": [str(path) for path in self.files],
 573 | |                 "warnings": list(self.warnings),
 574 | |                 "errors": list(self.errors),
 575 | |                 "started_at": self.started_at.isoformat(),
 576 | |                 "finished_at": (
 577 | |                     self.finished_at.isoformat() if self.finished_at else None
 578 | |                 ),
 579 | |             }
 580 | |
 581 | |     # =====================================================
 582 | |     # CLONE
 583 | |     # =====================================================
 584 | |
 585 | |     def clone(
 586 | |         self,
 587 | |     ) -> ExecutionContext:
 588 | |         """
 589 | |         Deep copy of the execution context.
 590 | |         """
 591 | |
 592 | |         clone = ExecutionContext()
 593 | |
 594 | |         with self._lock:
 595 | |             clone.metadata.update(self.metadata)
 596 | |
 597 | |             clone.outputs.update(self.outputs)
 598 | |
 599 | |             clone.variables.update(self.variables)
 600 | |
 601 | |             clone.cache.update(self.cache)
 602 | |
 603 | |             clone.artifacts.update(self.artifacts)
 604 | |
 605 | |             clone.files.extend(self.files)
 606 | |
 607 | |             clone.warnings.extend(self.warnings)
 608 | |
 609 | |             clone.errors.extend(self.errors)
 610 | |
 611 | |             clone.started_at = self.started_at
 612 | |
 613 | |             clone.finished_at = self.finished_at
 614 | |
 615 | |         return clone
 616 | |
 617 | |     # =====================================================
 618 | |     # MERGE
 619 | |     # =====================================================
 620 | |
 621 | |     def merge(
 622 | |         self,
 623 | |         other: ExecutionContext,
 624 | |     ) -> None:
 625 | |         """
 626 | |         Merge another execution context.
 627 | |         """
 628 | |
 629 | |         with self._lock:
 630 | |             self.metadata.update(other.metadata)
 631 | |
 632 | |             self.outputs.update(other.outputs)
 633 | |
 634 | |             self.variables.update(other.variables)
 635 | |
 636 | |             self.cache.update(other.cache)
 637 | |
 638 | |             self.artifacts.update(other.artifacts)
 639 | |
 640 | |             self.files.extend(other.files)
 641 | |
 642 | |             self.warnings.extend(other.warnings)
 643 | |
 644 | |             self.errors.extend(other.errors)
 645 | |
 646 | |     # =====================================================
 647 | |     # EXPORT
 648 | |     # =====================================================
 649 | |
 650 | |     def to_dict(
 651 | |         self,
 652 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 653 | |         """
 654 | |         Export execution context.
 655 | |         """
 656 | |
 657 | |         return self.snapshot()
 658 | |
 659 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 660 | |
 661 | |     def to_json(
 662 | |         self,
 663 | |         *,
 664 | |         indent: int = 4,
 665 | |     ) -> str:
 666 | |         """
 667 | |         JSON serialization.
 668 | |         """
 669 | |
 670 | |         return json.dumps(
 671 | |             self.to_dict(),
 672 | |             indent=indent,
 673 | |             default=str,
 674 | |         )
 675 | |
 676 | |     # =====================================================
 677 | |     # RESTORE
 678 | |     # =====================================================
 679 | |
 680 | |     @classmethod
 681 | |     def from_dict(
 682 | |         cls,
 683 | |         data: dict[str, Any],
 684 | |     ) -> ExecutionContext:
 685 | |         """
 686 | |         Restore execution context.
 687 | |         """
 688 | |
 689 | |         context = cls()
 690 | |
 691 | |         context.metadata.update(
 692 | |             data.get(
 693 | |                 "metadata",
 694 | |                 {},
 695 | |             )
 696 | |         )
 697 | |
 698 | |         context.outputs.update(
 699 | |             data.get(
 700 | |                 "outputs",
 701 | |                 {},
 702 | |             )
 703 | |         )
 704 | |
 705 | |         context.variables.update(
 706 | |             data.get(
 707 | |                 "variables",
 708 | |                 {},
 709 | |             )
 710 | |         )
 711 | |
 712 | |         context.cache.update(
 713 | |             data.get(
 714 | |                 "cache",
 715 | |                 {},
 716 | |             )
 717 | |         )
 718 | |
 719 | |         context.artifacts.update(
 720 | |             data.get(
 721 | |                 "artifacts",
 722 | |                 [],
 723 | |             )
 724 | |         )
 725 | |
 726 | |         context.files.extend(
 727 | |             Path(path)
 728 | |             for path in data.get(
 729 | |                 "files",
 730 | |                 [],
 731 | |             )
 732 | |         )
 733 | |
 734 | |         context.warnings.extend(
 735 | |             data.get(
 736 | |                 "warnings",
 737 | |                 [],
 738 | |             )
 739 | |         )
 740 | |
 741 | |         context.errors.extend(
 742 | |             data.get(
 743 | |                 "errors",
 744 | |                 [],
 745 | |             )
 746 | |         )
 747 | |
 748 | |         return context

 800 | |     def restore(
 801 | |         self,

 803 | |     ) -> None:
 804 | |         """
 805 | |         Restore execution state from a checkpoint.
 806 | |         """
 807 | |

 809 | |
 810 | |         with self._lock:
 811 | |             self.metadata = restored.metadata
 812 | |
 813 | |             self.outputs = restored.outputs
 814 | |
 815 | |             self.variables = restored.variables
 816 | |
 817 | |             self.cache = restored.cache
 818 | |
 819 | |             self.artifacts = restored.artifacts
 820 | |
 821 | |             self.files = restored.files
 822 | |
 823 | |             self.warnings = restored.warnings
 824 | |
 825 | |             self.errors = restored.errors
 826 | |
 827 | |             self.started_at = restored.started_at
 828 | |
 829 | |             self.finished_at = restored.finished_at
 830 | |
 831 | |     # =====================================================
 832 | |     # PERSISTENCE
 833 | |     # =====================================================
 834 | |
 835 | |     def save(
 836 | |         self,
 837 | |         path: Path,
 838 | |     ) -> None:
 839 | |         """
 840 | |         Save execution context to disk.
 841 | |         """
 842 | |
 843 | |         path.parent.mkdir(
 844 | |             parents=True,
 845 | |             exist_ok=True,
 846 | |         )
 847 | |
 848 | |         with path.open(
 849 | |             "w",
 850 | |             encoding="utf-8",
 851 | |         ) as fp:
 852 | |             json.dump(
 853 | |                 self.to_dict(),
 854 | |                 fp,
 855 | |                 indent=4,
 856 | |                 default=str,
 857 | |             )
 858 | |

 860 | |
 861 | |     @classmethod
 862 | |     def load(
 863 | |         cls,
 864 | |         path: Path,
 865 | |     ) -> ExecutionContext:
 866 | |         """
 867 | |         Load execution context.
 868 | |         """

 871 | |             "r",

 873 | |         ) as fp:
 874 | |             data = json.load(fp)

 907 | |     # DIFF
 908 | |     # =====================================================
 909 | |
 910 | |     def diff(
 911 | |         self,
 912 | |         other: ExecutionContext,
 913 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 914 | |         """
 915 | |         Compare two execution contexts.
 916 | |         """
 917 | |
 918 | |         return {
 919 | |             "metadata": set(self.metadata) ^ set(other.metadata),
 920 | |             "outputs": set(self.outputs) ^ set(other.outputs),
 921 | |             "variables": set(self.variables) ^ set(other.variables),
 922 | |             "artifacts": self.artifacts ^ other.artifacts,
 923 | |         }

 926 | |     # HASH
 927 | |     # =====================================================
 928 | |
 929 | |     def checksum(

 931 | |     ) -> str:
 932 | |         """
 933 | |         Stable checksum of the execution state.
 934 | |         """
 935 | |
 936 | |         return hashlib.sha256(self.to_json().encode()).hexdigest()
