 398 | |         self,
 399 | |         artifact: str,
 400 | |     ) -> None:
 401 | |
 402 | |         with self._lock:
 403 | |             self.artifacts.append(artifact)
 404 | |
 405 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 406 | |
 407 | |     def add_artifacts(
 408 | |         self,
 409 | |         artifacts: list[str],
 410 | |     ) -> None:
 411 | |
 412 | |         with self._lock:
 413 | |             self.artifacts.extend(artifacts)
 414 | |
 415 | |     # =====================================================
 416 | |     # WARNINGS
 417 | |     # =====================================================
 418 | |
 419 | |     def add_warning(
 420 | |         self,
 421 | |         warning: str,
 422 | |     ) -> None:
 423 | |
 424 | |         with self._lock:
 425 | |             self.warnings.append(warning)
 426 | |
 427 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 428 | |
 429 | |     def add_error(
 430 | |         self,
 431 | |         error: str,
 432 | |     ) -> None:
 433 | |
 434 | |         with self._lock:
 435 | |             self.errors.append(error)
 436 | |
 437 | |     # =====================================================
 438 | |     # MERGE
 439 | |     # =====================================================
 440 | |
 441 | |     def merge(
 442 | |         self,
 443 | |         other: ExecutionReport,
 444 | |     ) -> None:
 445 | |         """
 446 | |         Merge another report.
 447 | |         """
 448 | |
 449 | |         with self._lock:
 450 | |             self.engine_results.extend(other.engine_results)
 451 | |
 452 | |             self.pipeline_results.extend(other.pipeline_results)
 453 | |
 454 | |             self.outputs.extend(other.outputs)
 455 | |
 456 | |             self.artifacts.extend(other.artifacts)
 457 | |
 458 | |             self.warnings.extend(other.warnings)
 459 | |
 460 | |             self.errors.extend(other.errors)
 461 | |
 462 | |             self.metadata.update(other.metadata)
 463 | |
 464 | |             if other.master_result:
 465 | |                 self.master_result = other.master_result
 466 | |
 467 | |     # =====================================================
 468 | |     # CLEAR
 469 | |     # =====================================================
 470 | |
 471 | |     def clear(
 472 | |         self,
 473 | |     ) -> None:
 474 | |         """
 475 | |         Reset report.
 476 | |         """
 477 | |
 478 | |         with self._lock:
 479 | |             self.engine_results.clear()
 480 | |
 481 | |             self.pipeline_results.clear()
 482 | |
 483 | |             self.outputs.clear()
 484 | |
 485 | |             self.artifacts.clear()
 486 | |
 487 | |             self.warnings.clear()
 488 | |
 489 | |             self.errors.clear()
 490 | |
 491 | |             self.metadata.clear()
 492 | |
 493 | |             self.master_result = None
 494 | |
 495 | |             self.started_at = datetime.utcnow()
 496 | |
 497 | |             self.finished_at = None
 498 | |
 499 | |     # =====================================================
 500 | |     # SUCCESS RATES
 501 | |     # =====================================================
 502 | |
 503 | |     @property
 504 | |     def engine_success_rate(
 505 | |         self,
 506 | |     ) -> float:
 507 | |         """
 508 | |         Engine success percentage.
 509 | |         """
 510 | |
 511 | |         if not self.engine_results:
 512 | |             return 0.0
 513 | |
 514 | |         return round(
 515 | |             100.0 * self.successful_engines / self.total_engines,
 516 | |             2,
 517 | |         )
 518 | |
 519 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 520 | |
 521 | |     @property
 522 | |     def overall_status(
 523 | |         self,
 524 | |     ) -> EngineStatus:
 525 | |         """
 526 | |         Overall platform status.
 527 | |         """
 528 | |
 529 | |         if self.master_result:
 530 | |             return self.master_result.status
 531 | |
 532 | |         if self.failed_engines:
 533 | |             return EngineStatus.FAILED
 534 | |
 535 | |         if self.failed_pipelines:
 536 | |             return EngineStatus.FAILED
 537 | |
 538 | |         return EngineStatus.SUCCESS
 539 | |
 540 | |     # =====================================================
 541 | |     # RUNTIME ANALYTICS
 542 | |     # =====================================================
 543 | |
 544 | |     @property
 545 | |     def total_runtime(
 546 | |         self,
 547 | |     ) -> float:
 548 | |
 549 | |         return round(
 550 | |             sum(engine.duration for engine in self.engine_results),
 551 | |             3,
 552 | |         )
 553 | |
 554 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 555 | |
 556 | |     @property
 557 | |     def average_engine_runtime(
 558 | |         self,
 559 | |     ) -> float:
 560 | |
 561 | |         if not self.engine_results:
 562 | |             return 0.0
 563 | |
 564 | |         return round(
 565 | |             self.total_runtime / len(self.engine_results),
 566 | |             3,
 567 | |         )
 568 | |
 569 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 570 | |
 571 | |     @property
 572 | |     def average_pipeline_runtime(
 573 | |         self,
 574 | |     ) -> float:
 575 | |
 576 | |         if not self.pipeline_results:
 577 | |             return 0.0
 578 | |
 579 | |         return round(
 580 | |             sum(pipeline.duration for pipeline in self.pipeline_results)
 581 | |             / len(self.pipeline_results),
 582 | |             3,
 583 | |         )
 584 | |
 585 | |     # =====================================================
 586 | |     # ENGINE RANKINGS
 587 | |     # =====================================================
 588 | |
 589 | |     def slowest_engines(
 590 | |         self,
 591 | |         limit: int = 10,
 592 | |     ) -> list[EngineResult]:
 593 | |         """
 594 | |         Slowest executed engines.
 595 | |         """
 596 | |
 597 | |         return sorted(
 598 | |             self.engine_results,
 599 | |             key=lambda result: result.duration,
 600 | |             reverse=True,
 601 | |         )[:limit]
 602 | |
 603 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 604 | |
 605 | |     def fastest_engines(
 606 | |         self,
 607 | |         limit: int = 10,
 608 | |     ) -> list[EngineResult]:
 609 | |         """
 610 | |         Fastest engines.
 611 | |         """
 612 | |
 613 | |         return sorted(
 614 | |             self.engine_results,
 615 | |             key=lambda result: result.duration,
 616 | |         )[:limit]
 617 | |
 618 | |     # =====================================================
 619 | |     # PIPELINE RANKINGS
 620 | |     # =====================================================
 621 | |
 622 | |     def slowest_pipelines(
 623 | |         self,
 624 | |         limit: int = 10,
 625 | |     ) -> list[PipelineResult]:
 626 | |
 627 | |         return sorted(
 628 | |             self.pipeline_results,
 629 | |             key=lambda result: result.duration,
 630 | |             reverse=True,
 631 | |         )[:limit]
 632 | |
 633 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 634 | |
 635 | |     def fastest_pipelines(
 636 | |         self,
 637 | |         limit: int = 10,
 638 | |     ) -> list[PipelineResult]:
 639 | |
 640 | |         return sorted(
 641 | |             self.pipeline_results,
 642 | |             key=lambda result: result.duration,
 643 | |         )[:limit]
 644 | |
 645 | |     # =====================================================
 646 | |     # FAILURES
 647 | |     # =====================================================
 648 | |
 649 | |     def failed_engine_results(
 650 | |         self,
 651 | |     ) -> list[EngineResult]:
 652 | |
 653 | |         return [
 654 | |             result
 655 | |             for result in self.engine_results
 656 | |             if (result.status == EngineStatus.FAILED)
 657 | |         ]
 658 | |
 659 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 660 | |
 661 | |     def failed_pipeline_results(
 662 | |         self,
 663 | |     ) -> list[PipelineResult]:
 664 | |
 665 | |         return [
 666 | |             result
 667 | |             for result in self.pipeline_results
 668 | |             if (result.status == EngineStatus.FAILED)
 669 | |         ]
 670 | |
 671 | |     # =====================================================
 672 | |     # TIMELINE
 673 | |     # =====================================================
 674 | |
 675 | |     def timeline(
 676 | |         self,
 677 | |     ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
 678 | |         """
 679 | |         Execution timeline.
 680 | |         """
 681 | |
 682 | |         timeline = []
 683 | |
 684 | |         for engine in self.engine_results:
 685 | |             timeline.append(
 686 | |                 {
 687 | |                     "type": "engine",
 688 | |                     "name": engine.engine,
 689 | |                     "status": engine.status.value,
 690 | |                     "duration": engine.duration,
 691 | |                 }
 692 | |             )
 693 | |
 694 | |         for pipeline in self.pipeline_results:
 695 | |             timeline.append(
 696 | |                 {
 697 | |                     "type": "pipeline",
 698 | |                     "name": pipeline.pipeline,
 699 | |                     "status": pipeline.status.value,
 700 | |                     "duration": pipeline.duration,
 701 | |                 }
 702 | |             )
 703 | |
 704 | |         return timeline
 705 | |
 706 | |     # =====================================================
 707 | |     # STATISTICS
 708 | |     # =====================================================
 709 | |
 710 | |     def statistics(
 711 | |         self,
 712 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 713 | |
 714 | |         return {
 715 | |             "runtime": round(
 716 | |                 self.runtime_seconds,
 717 | |                 3,
 718 | |             ),
 719 | |             "engine_success_rate": self.engine_success_rate,
 720 | |             "pipeline_success_rate": self.pipeline_success_rate,
 721 | |             "engines": self.total_engines,
 722 | |             "pipelines": self.total_pipelines,
 723 | |             "successful_engines": self.successful_engines,
 724 | |             "failed_engines": self.failed_engines,
 725 | |             "successful_pipelines": self.successful_pipelines,
 726 | |             "failed_pipelines": self.failed_pipelines,
 727 | |             "warnings": len(self.warnings),
 728 | |             "errors": len(self.errors),
 729 | |             "outputs": len(self.outputs),
 730 | |             "artifacts": len(self.artifacts),
 731 | |         }
 732 | |
 733 | |     # =====================================================
 734 | |     # AUDIT REPORT
 735 | |     # =====================================================
 736 | |
 737 | |     def audit_report(
 738 | |         self,
 739 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 740 | |         """
 741 | |         Comprehensive execution audit.
 742 | |         """
 743 | |
 744 | |         return {
 745 | |             "summary": self.summary(),
 746 | |             "statistics": self.statistics(),
 747 | |             "timeline": self.timeline(),
 748 | |             "metadata": self.metadata,
 749 | |             "warnings": self.warnings,
 750 | |             "errors": self.errors,
 751 | |             "outputs": self.outputs,
 752 | |             "artifacts": self.artifacts,
 753 | |         }
 754 | |
 755 | |     # =====================================================
 756 | |     # EXPORT
 757 | |     # =====================================================
 758 | |
 759 | |     def to_dict(
 760 | |         self,
 761 | |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 762 | |         """
 763 | |         Convert execution report to dictionary.
 764 | |         """
 765 | |
 766 | |         return {
 767 | |             "started_at": self.started_at.isoformat(),
 768 | |             "finished_at": self.finished_at.isoformat() if self.finished_at else None,
 769 | |             "runtime": round(
 770 | |                 self.runtime_seconds,
 771 | |                 3,
 772 | |             ),
 773 | |             "master_result": (
 774 | |                 self.master_result.to_dict() if self.master_result else None
 775 | |             ),
 776 | |             "statistics": self.statistics(),
 777 | |             "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
 778 | |             "outputs": list(self.outputs),
 779 | |             "artifacts": list(self.artifacts),
 780 | |             "warnings": list(self.warnings),
 781 | |             "errors": list(self.errors),
 782 | |             "engines": [engine.to_dict() for engine in self.engine_results],
 783 | |             "pipelines": [pipeline.to_dict() for pipeline in self.pipeline_results],
 784 | |         }
 785 | |
 786 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 787 | |
 788 | |     def to_json(
 789 | |         self,
 790 | |         *,
 791 | |         indent: int = 4,
 792 | |     ) -> str:
 793 | |         """
 794 | |         JSON serialization.
 795 | |         """
 796 | |
 797 | |         return json.dumps(
 798 | |             self.to_dict(),
 799 | |             indent=indent,
 800 | |             default=str,
 801 | |         )
 802 | |
 803 | |     # =====================================================
 804 | |     # PERSISTENCE
 805 | |     # =====================================================
 806 | |
 807 | |     def save(
 808 | |         self,
 809 | |         path: Path,
 810 | |     ) -> None:
 811 | |         """
 812 | |         Save report to disk.
 813 | |         """
 814 | |
 815 | |         path.parent.mkdir(
 816 | |             parents=True,
 817 | |             exist_ok=True,
 818 | |         )
 819 | |
 820 | |         path.write_text(
 821 | |             self.to_json(),
 822 | |             encoding="utf-8",
 823 | |         )
 824 | |
 825 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 826 | |
 827 | |     @classmethod
 828 | |     def load(
 829 | |         cls,
 830 | |         path: Path,
 831 | |     ) -> ExecutionReport:
 832 | |         """
 833 | |         Restore report from JSON.
 834 | |
 835 | |         NOTE:
 836 | |         EngineResult/PipelineResult reconstruction
 837 | |         requires their corresponding from_dict()
 838 | |         implementations.
 839 | |         """
 840 | |
 841 | |         data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 842 | |
 843 | |         report = cls()
 844 | |
 845 | |         report.metadata.update(
 846 | |             data.get(
 847 | |                 "metadata",
 848 | |                 {},
 849 | |             )
 850 | |         )
 851 | |
 852 | |         report.outputs.extend(
 853 | |             data.get(
 854 | |                 "outputs",
 855 | |                 [],
 856 | |             )
 857 | |         )
 858 | |
 859 | |         report.artifacts.extend(
 860 | |             data.get(
 861 | |                 "artifacts",
 862 | |                 [],
 863 | |             )
 864 | |         )
 865 | |
 866 | |         report.warnings.extend(
 867 | |             data.get(
 868 | |                 "warnings",
 869 | |                 [],
 870 | |             )
 871 | |         )
 872 | |
 873 | |         report.errors.extend(
 874 | |             data.get(
 875 | |                 "errors",
 876 | |                 [],
 877 | |             )
 878 | |         )
 879 | |
 880 | |         return report
 881 | |
 882 | |     # =====================================================
 883 | |     # MARKDOWN
 884 | |     # =====================================================
 885 | |
 886 | |     def to_markdown(
 887 | |         self,
 888 | |     ) -> str:
 889 | |         """
 890 | |         Generate Markdown execution report.
 891 | |         """
 892 | |
 893 | |         lines = [
 894 | |             "# Execution Report",
 895 | |             "",
 896 | |             f"**Runtime:** {self.runtime_seconds:.2f}s",
 897 | |             f"**Status:** {self.overall_status.value}",
 898 | |             "",
 899 | |             "## Statistics",
 900 | |             "",
 901 | |         ]
 902 | |
 903 | |         for key, value in self.statistics().items():
 904 | |             lines.append(f"- **{key}** : {value}")
 905 | |
 906 | |         lines.extend(
 907 | |             [
 908 | |                 "",
 909 | |                 "## Pipelines",
 910 | |                 "",
 911 | |             ]
 912 | |         )
 913 | |
 914 | |         for pipeline in self.pipeline_results:
 915 | |             lines.append(
 916 | |                 f"- {pipeline.pipeline}"
 917 | |                 f" ({pipeline.status.value})"
 918 | |                 f" [{pipeline.duration:.2f}s]"
 919 | |             )
 920 | |
 921 | |         lines.extend(
 922 | |             [
 923 | |                 "",
 924 | |                 "## Engines",
 925 | |                 "",
 926 | |             ]
 927 | |         )
 928 | |
 929 | |         for engine in self.engine_results:
 930 | |             lines.append(
 931 | |                 f"- {engine.engine} ({engine.status.value}) [{engine.duration:.2f}s]"
 932 | |             )
 933 | |
 934 | |         return "\n".join(lines)
 935 | |
 936 | |     # =====================================================
 937 | |     # CSV
 938 | |     # =====================================================
 939 | |
 940 | |     def export_csv(
 941 | |         self,
 942 | |         path: Path,
 943 | |     ) -> None:
 944 | |         """
 945 | |         Export engine execution results.
 946 | |         """
 947 | |
 948 | |         path.parent.mkdir(
 949 | |             parents=True,
 950 | |             exist_ok=True,
 951 | |         )
 952 | |
 953 | |         with path.open(
 954 | |             "w",
 955 | |             newline="",
 956 | |             encoding="utf-8",
 957 | |         ) as fp:
 958 | |             writer = csv.writer(fp)
 959 | |
 960 | |             writer.writerow(
 961 | |                 [
 962 | |                     "Engine",
 963 | |                     "Status",
 964 | |                     "Duration",
 965 | |                 ]
 966 | |             )
 967 | |
 968 | |             for result in self.engine_results:
 969 | |                 writer.writerow(
 970 | |                     [
 971 | |                         result.engine,
 972 | |                         result.status.value,
 973 | |                         result.duration,
 974 | |                     ]
 975 | |                 )

 102 | |     """
 103 | |     Enterprise cache manager.
 104 | |
 105 | |     Thread-safe singleton.
 106 | |
 107 | |     Supports
 108 | |
 109 | |     • Memory cache
 110 | |     • TTL
 111 | |     • LRU
 112 | |     • Statistics
 113 | |     • Persistence
 114 | |     """
 115 | |
 116 | |     _instance = None
 117 | |
 118 | |     _instance_lock = Lock()
 119 | |
 120 | |     # --------------------------------------------------------
 121 | |
 122 | |     def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
 123 | |
 124 | |         if cls._instance is None:
 125 | |             with cls._instance_lock:
 126 | |                 if cls._instance is None:
 127 | |                     cls._instance = super().__new__(cls)
 128 | |
 129 | |         return cls._instance
 130 | |
 131 | |     # --------------------------------------------------------
 132 | |
 133 | |     def __init__(self, max_entries: int = 10000):
 134 | |
 135 | |         if getattr(self, "_initialized", False):
 136 | |             return
 137 | |
 138 | |         super().__init__()
 139 | |
 140 | |         self._cache = OrderedDict()
 141 | |
 142 | |         self._lock = RLock()
 143 | |
 144 | |         self._max_entries = max_entries
 145 | |
 146 | |         self._enabled = True
 147 | |
 148 | |         self._default_ttl = None
 149 | |
 150 | |         self._hits = 0
 151 | |

 153 | |

 156 | |         self._writes = 0
 157 | |
 158 | |         self._deletes = 0
 159 | |
 160 | |         self._cleanups = 0
 161 | |
 162 | |         self._initialized = True
 163 | |
 164 | |         self._logger.info("CacheService initialized.")
 165 | |

 167 | |     # Lifecycle
 168 | |     # =====================================================
 169 | |

 171 | |
 172 | |         self._enabled = True
 173 | |
 174 | |     def disable(self):
 175 | |
 176 | |         self._enabled = False
 177 | |
 178 | |     def enabled(self):
 179 | |
 180 | |         return self._enabled
 181 | |
 182 | |     # =====================================================
 183 | |     # Configuration
 184 | |     # =====================================================
 185 | |
 186 | |     def set_default_ttl(self, seconds: int | None):
 187 | |
 188 | |         self._default_ttl = seconds
 189 | |
 190 | |     def default_ttl(self):
 191 | |
 192 | |         return self._default_ttl
 193 | |
 194 | |     def capacity(self):
 195 | |
 196 | |         return self._max_entries
 197 | |
 198 | |     def size(self):
 199 | |
 200 | |         return len(self._cache)
 201 | |
 202 | |     def empty(self):
 203 | |
 204 | |         return len(self._cache) == 0
 205 | |
 206 | |     # =====================================================
 207 | |     # Internal Helpers
 208 | |     # =====================================================
 209 | |
 210 | |     def _ensure_capacity(self):
 211 | |         """
 212 | |         LRU eviction.
 213 | |         """
 214 | |
 215 | |         while len(self._cache) > self._max_entries:
 216 | |             key, entry = self._cache.popitem(last=False)
 217 | |
 218 | |             self._notify_eviction(key, entry.value)
 219 | |
 220 | |             self._evictions += 1
 221 | |
 222 | |     def _cleanup_expired(self):
 223 | |         """
 224 | |         Remove expired keys.
 225 | |         """
 226 | |
 227 | |         expired = []
 228 | |
 229 | |         for key, entry in self._cache.items():
 230 | |             if entry.expired:
 231 | |                 expired.append(key)
 232 | |
 233 | |         for key in expired:
 234 | |             del self._cache[key]
 235 | |
 236 | |         self._cleanups += len(expired)
 237 | |
 238 | |     # =====================================================
 239 | |     # BaseService
 240 | |     # =====================================================
 241 | |
 242 | |     def run(self):
 243 | |
 244 | |         return self.statistics()
 245 | |
 246 | |     # =====================================================
 247 | |     # Core Cache Operations
 248 | |     # =====================================================
 249 | |
 250 | |     def put(
 251 | |         self,
 252 | |         key: str,
 253 | |         value: Any,
 254 | |         ttl: int | None = None,
 255 | |         metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
 256 | |     ) -> None:
 257 | |         """
 258 | |         Store an item in cache.
 259 | |         """
 260 | |
 261 | |         if not self._enabled:
 262 | |             return
 263 | |
 264 | |         if ttl is None:
 265 | |             ttl = self._default_ttl
 266 | |
 267 | |         with self._lock:
 268 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 269 | |
 270 | |             entry = CacheEntry(key=key, value=value, ttl=ttl, metadata=metadata or {})
 271 | |
 272 | |             self._cache[key] = entry
 273 | |
 274 | |             self._cache.move_to_end(key, last=True)
 275 | |
 276 | |             self._writes += 1
 277 | |
 278 | |             self._ensure_capacity()
 279 | |
 280 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 281 | |
 282 | |     def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
 283 | |         """
 284 | |         Retrieve an item from cache.
 285 | |         """
 286 | |
 287 | |         if not self._enabled:
 288 | |             return default
 289 | |
 290 | |         with self._lock:
 291 | |             entry = self._cache.get(key)
 292 | |
 293 | |             if entry is None:
 294 | |                 self._misses += 1
 295 | |
 296 | |                 return default
 297 | |
 298 | |             if entry.expired:
 299 | |                 del self._cache[key]
 300 | |
 301 | |                 self._misses += 1
 302 | |
 303 | |                 return default
 304 | |
 305 | |             entry.touch()
 306 | |
 307 | |             self._cache.move_to_end(key, last=True)
 308 | |
 309 | |             self._hits += 1
 310 | |
 311 | |             return entry.value
 312 | |
 313 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 314 | |
 315 | |     def require(self, key: str) -> Any:
 316 | |         """
 317 | |         Retrieve a required cache value.
 318 | |         """
 319 | |
 320 | |         value = self.get(key, default=None)
 321 | |
 322 | |         if value is None:
 323 | |             raise CacheKeyNotFoundError(f"'{key}' not found.")
 324 | |
 325 | |         return value
 326 | |
 327 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 328 | |
 329 | |     def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
 330 | |         """
 331 | |         Delete cache key.
 332 | |         """
 333 | |
 334 | |         with self._lock:
 335 | |             if key not in self._cache:
 336 | |                 return False
 337 | |
 338 | |             del self._cache[key]
 339 | |
 340 | |             self._deletes += 1
 341 | |
 342 | |             return True
 343 | |
 344 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 345 | |
 346 | |     def clear(self) -> None:
 347 | |         """
 348 | |         Clear cache.
 349 | |         """
 350 | |
 351 | |         with self._lock:
 352 | |             self._cache.clear()
 353 | |
 354 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 355 | |
 356 | |     def contains(self, key: str) -> bool:
 357 | |
 358 | |         with self._lock:
 359 | |             entry = self._cache.get(key)
 360 | |
 361 | |             if entry is None:
 362 | |                 return False
 363 | |
 364 | |             if entry.expired:
 365 | |                 del self._cache[key]
 366 | |
 367 | |                 return False
 368 | |
 369 | |             return True
 370 | |
 371 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 372 | |
 373 | |     def pop(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
 374 | |         """
 375 | |         Remove and return cache value.
 376 | |         """
 377 | |
 378 | |         with self._lock:
 379 | |             entry = self._cache.pop(key, None)
 380 | |
 381 | |             if entry is None:
 382 | |                 return default
 383 | |
 384 | |             return entry.value
 385 | |
 386 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 387 | |
 388 | |     def touch(self, key: str) -> bool:
 389 | |         """
 390 | |         Refresh access timestamp.
 391 | |         """
 392 | |
 393 | |         with self._lock:
 394 | |             entry = self._cache.get(key)
 395 | |
 396 | |             if entry is None:
 397 | |                 return False
 398 | |
 399 | |             if entry.expired:
 400 | |                 del self._cache[key]
 401 | |
 402 | |                 return False
 403 | |
 404 | |             entry.touch()
 405 | |
 406 | |             self._cache.move_to_end(key, last=True)
 407 | |
 408 | |             return True
 409 | |
 410 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 411 | |
 412 | |     def update(self, key: str, value: Any) -> bool:
 413 | |         """
 414 | |         Update existing cache entry.
 415 | |         """
 416 | |
 417 | |         with self._lock:
 418 | |             entry = self._cache.get(key)
 419 | |
 420 | |             if entry is None:
 421 | |                 return False
 422 | |
 423 | |             if entry.expired:
 424 | |                 del self._cache[key]
 425 | |
 426 | |                 return False
 427 | |
 428 | |             entry.value = value
 429 | |
 430 | |             entry.touch()
 431 | |
 432 | |             self._cache.move_to_end(key, last=True)
 433 | |
 434 | |             return True
 435 | |
 436 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 437 | |
 438 | |     def get_or_create(
 439 | |         self, key: str, factory: Callable[[], Any], ttl: int | None = None
 440 | |     ) -> Any:
 441 | |         """
 442 | |         Return cached value or create it.
 443 | |         """
 444 | |
 445 | |         value = self.get(key, default=None)
 446 | |
 447 | |         if value is not None:
 448 | |             return value
 449 | |
 450 | |         value = factory()
 451 | |
 452 | |         self.put(key, value, ttl=ttl)
 453 | |
 454 | |         return value
 455 | |
 456 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 457 | |
 458 | |     def keys(self) -> list[str]:
 459 | |
 460 | |         with self._lock:
 461 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 462 | |
 463 | |             return list(self._cache.keys())
 464 | |
 465 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 466 | |
 467 | |     def values(self) -> list[Any]:
 468 | |
 469 | |         with self._lock:
 470 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 471 | |
 472 | |             return [entry.value for entry in self._cache.values()]
 473 | |
 474 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 475 | |
 476 | |     def items(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 477 | |
 478 | |         with self._lock:
 479 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 480 | |
 481 | |             return {key: entry.value for key, entry in self._cache.items()}
 482 | |
 483 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 484 | |
 485 | |     def __contains__(self, key: str) -> bool:
 486 | |
 487 | |         return self.contains(key)
 488 | |
 489 | |     def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> Any:
 490 | |
 491 | |         return self.require(key)
 492 | |
 493 | |     def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
 494 | |
 495 | |         self.put(key, value)
 496 | |

 498 | |

 501 | |     def __len__(self) -> int:
 502 | |
 503 | |         return self.size()
 504 | |
 505 | |     # =====================================================
 506 | |     # Statistics
 507 | |     # =====================================================
 508 | |
 509 | |     @property
 510 | |     def hits(self) -> int:
 511 | |
 512 | |         return self._hits
 513 | |
 514 | |     @property
 515 | |     def misses(self) -> int:
 516 | |
 517 | |         return self._misses
 518 | |
 519 | |     @property
 520 | |     def writes(self) -> int:
 521 | |
 522 | |         return self._writes
 523 | |
 524 | |     @property
 525 | |     def deletes(self) -> int:
 526 | |
 527 | |         return self._deletes
 528 | |
 529 | |     @property
 530 | |     def evictions(self) -> int:
 531 | |
 532 | |         return self._evictions
 533 | |
 534 | |     @property
 535 | |     def cleanups(self) -> int:
 536 | |
 537 | |         return self._cleanups
 538 | |
 539 | |     @property
 540 | |     def requests(self) -> int:
 541 | |
 542 | |         return self._hits + self._misses
 543 | |
 544 | |     @property
 545 | |     def hit_ratio(self) -> float:
 546 | |
 547 | |         requests = self.requests
 548 | |
 549 | |         if requests == 0:
 550 | |             return 0.0
 551 | |
 552 | |         return self._hits / requests
 553 | |
 554 | |     @property
 555 | |     def miss_ratio(self) -> float:
 556 | |
 557 | |         requests = self.requests
 558 | |
 559 | |         if requests == 0:
 560 | |             return 0.0
 561 | |
 562 | |         return self._misses / requests
 563 | |
 564 | |     # =====================================================
 565 | |     # Statistics API
 566 | |     # =====================================================
 567 | |
 568 | |     def statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 569 | |         """
 570 | |         Cache statistics.
 571 | |         """
 572 | |
 573 | |         return {
 574 | |             "enabled": self._enabled,
 575 | |             "entries": len(self._cache),
 576 | |             "capacity": self._max_entries,
 577 | |             "utilization": (
 578 | |                 len(self._cache) / self._max_entries if self._max_entries else 0.0
 579 | |             ),
 580 | |             "hits": self._hits,
 581 | |             "misses": self._misses,
 582 | |             "requests": self.requests,
 583 | |             "writes": self._writes,
 584 | |             "deletes": self._deletes,
 585 | |             "evictions": self._evictions,
 586 | |             "cleanups": self._cleanups,
 587 | |             "hit_ratio": round(self.hit_ratio, 4),
 588 | |             "miss_ratio": round(self.miss_ratio, 4),
 589 | |         }
 590 | |
 591 | |     # =====================================================
 592 | |     # Export
 593 | |     # =====================================================
 594 | |
 595 | |     def export_json(self, file: Path) -> Path:
 596 | |         """
 597 | |         Export cache contents as JSON.
 598 | |         """
 599 | |
 600 | |         payload = {}
 601 | |
 602 | |         with self._lock:
 603 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 604 | |
 605 | |             for key, entry in self._cache.items():
 606 | |                 payload[key] = {
 607 | |                     "value": entry.value,
 608 | |                     "created_at": entry.created_at,
 609 | |                     "accessed_at": entry.accessed_at,
 610 | |                     "ttl": entry.ttl,
 611 | |                     "hits": entry.hits,
 612 | |                     "metadata": entry.metadata,
 613 | |                 }
 614 | |
 615 | |         file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 616 | |
 617 | |         with file.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
 618 | |             json.dump(payload, handle, indent=4, default=str)
 619 | |
 620 | |         return file
 621 | |
 622 | |     # =====================================================
 623 | |     # Pickle Export
 624 | |     # =====================================================
 625 | |
 626 | |     def save(self, file: Path) -> Path:
 627 | |         """
 628 | |         Persist cache.
 629 | |         """
 630 | |
 631 | |         file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 632 | |
 633 | |         with file.open("wb") as handle:
 634 | |             pickle.dump(self._cache, handle, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
 635 | |
 636 | |         return file
 637 | |
 638 | |     def load(self, file: Path) -> None:
 639 | |         """
 640 | |         Restore cache.
 641 | |         """
 642 | |
 643 | |         if not file.exists():
 644 | |             raise FileNotFoundError(file)
 645 | |
 646 | |         with file.open("rb") as handle:
 647 | |             cache = pickle.load(handle)
 648 | |
 649 | |         if not isinstance(cache, OrderedDict):
 650 | |             raise CacheSerializationError("Invalid cache file.")
 651 | |
 652 | |         with self._lock:
 653 | |             self._cache = cache
 654 | |
 655 | |     # =====================================================
 656 | |     # Snapshot
 657 | |     # =====================================================
 658 | |
 659 | |     def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 660 | |         """
 661 | |         Return complete cache snapshot.
 662 | |         """
 663 | |
 664 | |         with self._lock:
 665 | |             self._cleanup_expired()
 666 | |
 667 | |             snapshot = {}
 668 | |
 669 | |             for key, entry in self._cache.items():
 670 | |                 snapshot[key] = {

 672 | |                     "ttl": entry.ttl,
 673 | |                     "hits": entry.hits,
 674 | |                     "metadata": dict(entry.metadata),
 675 | |                 }
 676 | |
 677 | |             return snapshot

 679 | |     # =====================================================
 680 | |     # Diagnostics
 681 | |     # =====================================================
 682 | |
 683 | |     def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
 684 | |         """
 685 | |         Cache health report.
 686 | |         """
 687 | |
 688 | |         utilization = len(self._cache) / self._max_entries
 689 | |
 690 | |         status = "HEALTHY"
 691 | |

 693 | |             status = "CRITICAL"

 695 | |         elif utilization >= 0.80:
 696 | |             status = "WARNING"
 697 | |
 698 | |         return {

 700 | |             "enabled": self._enabled,
 701 | |             "entries": len(self._cache),
 702 | |             "capacity": self._max_entries,
 703 | |             "utilization": round(utilization, 4),
 704 | |             "hit_ratio": round(self.hit_ratio, 4),
 705 | |         }

 744 | |     def put_many(self, entries: dict[str, Any], ttl: int | None = None) -> None:

 747 | |         """

 753 | |
 754 | |     def get_many(self, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 755 | |         """
 756 | |         Retrieve multiple cache entries.
 757 | |         """
 758 | |
 759 | |         results: dict[str, Any] = {}
 760 | |
 761 | |         for key in keys:
 762 | |             value = self.get(key, default=None)
 763 | |
 764 | |             if value is not None:
 765 | |                 results[key] = value
 766 | |
 767 | |         return results
 768 | |
 769 | |     # -----------------------------------------------------
 770 | |
 771 | |     def delete_many(self, keys: list[str]) -> int:
 772 | |         """
 773 | |         Delete multiple cache entries.
 774 | |         """
 775 | |
 776 | |         deleted = 0
 777 | |
 778 | |         for key in keys:
 779 | |             if self.delete(key):
 780 | |                 deleted += 1
 781 | |
 782 | |         return deleted

 803 | |     def clear_namespace(self, namespace: str) -> int:
 804 | |         """
 805 | |         Remove an entire namespace.
 806 | |         """
 807 | |
 808 | |         prefix = f"{namespace}:"

 814 | |
 815 | |             for key in keys:
 816 | |                 del self._cache[key]
 817 | |

 822 | |     # =====================================================
 823 | |     # Decorator
 824 | |     # =====================================================
 825 | |
 826 | |     def cached(self, ttl: int | None = None, namespace: str = "default"):
 827 | |         """
 828 | |         Cache decorator.
 829 | |         """
 830 | |
 831 | |         def decorator(function):
 832 | |
 833 | |             def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
 834 | |
 835 | |                 cache_key = (
 836 | |                     f"{namespace}:"
 837 | |                     f"{function.__module__}."
 838 | |                     f"{function.__name__}:"
 839 | |                     f"{args!r}:"
 840 | |                     f"{kwargs!r}"
 841 | |                 )
 842 | |
 843 | |                 value = self.get(cache_key, default=None)
 844 | |
 845 | |                 if value is not None:
 846 | |                     return value
 847 | |
 848 | |                 value = function(*args, **kwargs)
 849 | |
 850 | |                 self.put(cache_key, value, ttl)
 851 | |
 852 | |                 return value
 853 | |
 854 | |             return wrapper
 855 | |
 856 | |         return decorator

192 | |         """
193 | |         Register additional configuration section.
194 | |         """
195 | |
196 | |         with self._lock:
197 | |             self._configuration_registry[name] = configuration
198 | |
199 | |             self._registered_sections.add(name)
200 | |
201 | |     # ========================================================
202 | |     # Runtime Overrides
203 | |     # ========================================================
204 | |
205 | |     def set_runtime_override(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
206 | |         """
207 | |         Override a configuration value at runtime.
208 | |         """
209 | |
210 | |         with self._lock:
211 | |             self._runtime_overrides[key] = value
212 | |
213 | |             self._logger.info("Runtime override applied: %s", key)
214 | |
215 | |     def clear_runtime_override(self, key: str) -> None:
216 | |         """
217 | |         Remove runtime override.
218 | |         """
219 | |
220 | |         with self._lock:
221 | |             self._runtime_overrides.pop(key, None)
222 | |
223 | |     def clear_all_runtime_overrides(self) -> None:
224 | |         """
225 | |         Remove every runtime override.
226 | |         """
227 | |
228 | |         with self._lock:
229 | |             self._runtime_overrides.clear()
230 | |
231 | |     # ========================================================

261 | |     # ========================================================
262 | |     # Configuration Lookup
263 | |     # ========================================================
264 | |
265 | |     def get(self, section: str, key: str | None = None, default: Any = None) -> Any:
266 | |         """
267 | |         Retrieve a configuration value.
268 | |
269 | |         Resolution Order
270 | |         ----------------
271 | |         1. Runtime Override
272 | |         2. Environment Variable
273 | |         3. core.settings
274 | |         4. Default
275 | |         """
276 | |
277 | |         section = section.lower()
278 | |
279 | |         if section not in self._configuration_registry:
280 | |             if default is not None:
281 | |                 return default
282 | |
283 | |             raise ConfigurationNotFoundError(
284 | |                 f"Unknown configuration section '{section}'."
285 | |             )
286 | |
287 | |         configuration = self._configuration_registry[section]
288 | |
289 | |         if key is None:
290 | |             return configuration
291 | |
292 | |         override_key = f"{section}.{key}"
293 | |
294 | |         if override_key in self._runtime_overrides:
295 | |             return self._runtime_overrides[override_key]
296 | |
297 | |         env_key = override_key.upper().replace(".", "_")
298 | |
299 | |         env_value = os.getenv(env_key)
300 | |
301 | |         if env_value is not None:
302 | |             return self._cast_environment_value(env_value)
303 | |
304 | |         if hasattr(configuration, key):
305 | |             return getattr(configuration, key)
306 | |
307 | |         if default is not None:
308 | |             return default
309 | |
310 | |         raise ConfigurationNotFoundError(f"Configuration '{override_key}' not found.")
311 | |
312 | |     # ========================================================
313 | |     # Exists
314 | |     # ========================================================
315 | |
316 | |     def exists(self, section: str, key: str | None = None) -> bool:
317 | |         """
318 | |         Determine whether a configuration exists.
319 | |         """
320 | |
321 | |         section = section.lower()
322 | |
323 | |         if section not in self._configuration_registry:
324 | |             return False
325 | |
326 | |         if key is None:
327 | |             return True
328 | |
329 | |         configuration = self._configuration_registry[section]
330 | |
331 | |         return hasattr(configuration, key)
332 | |

462 | |     # ========================================================
463 | |     # Internal Helpers
464 | |     # ========================================================
465 | |
466 | |     @staticmethod
467 | |     def _cast_environment_value(value: str) -> Any:
468 | |         """
469 | |         Convert environment variables into native types.
470 | |         """
471 | |
472 | |         lowered = value.lower()
473 | |
474 | |         if lowered in {"true", "false"}:
475 | |             return lowered == "true"
476 | |
477 | |         try:
478 | |             return int(value)
479 | |
480 | |         except ValueError:
481 | |             pass
482 | |
483 | |         try:
484 | |             return float(value)
485 | |
486 | |         except ValueError:
487 | |             pass
488 | |
489 | |         return value
490 | |

496 | |         """
497 | |         Validate every registered configuration section.
498 | |         """
499 | |
500 | |         for _section, validator in self._validators.items():
501 | |             validator()
502 | |
503 | |         self._logger.info("Configuration validation completed successfully.")
504 | |

507 | |     def _validate_environment(self) -> None:
508 | |
509 | |         environment = self.get_environment()
510 | |
511 | |         if environment.ENVIRONMENT not in {
512 | |             "DEVELOPMENT",
513 | |             "TESTING",
514 | |             "UAT",
515 | |             "PRODUCTION",
516 | |         }:
517 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError(
518 | |                 f"Invalid environment '{environment.ENVIRONMENT}'."
519 | |             )

521 | |     def _validate_portfolio(self) -> None:
522 | |
523 | |         portfolio = self.get_portfolio()
524 | |
525 | |         if portfolio.MIN_POSITION_WEIGHT <= 0:
526 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError(
527 | |                 "Minimum position weight must be positive."
528 | |             )
529 | |
530 | |         if portfolio.MAX_POSITION_WEIGHT <= portfolio.MIN_POSITION_WEIGHT:
531 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError(
532 | |                 "Maximum position weight must exceed minimum position weight."
533 | |             )
534 | |
535 | |         if portfolio.TARGET_HOLDINGS <= 0:
536 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError(
537 | |                 "Target holdings must be greater than zero."
538 | |             )
539 | |
540 | |     def _validate_risk(self) -> None:
541 | |
542 | |         risk = self.get_risk()
543 | |
544 | |         if risk.MIN_BETA >= risk.MAX_BETA:
545 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError("MIN_BETA must be less than MAX_BETA.")
546 | |
547 | |         if not (0.0 < risk.VAR_CONFIDENCE < 1.0):
548 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError(
549 | |                 "VAR_CONFIDENCE must be between 0 and 1."
550 | |             )
551 | |
552 | |     def _validate_execution(self) -> None:
553 | |
554 | |         execution = self.get_execution()
555 | |
556 | |         if execution.MAX_PARTICIPATION_RATE <= 0:
557 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError("Participation rate must be positive.")
558 | |
559 | |     def _validate_governance(self) -> None:
560 | |
561 | |         governance = self.get_governance()
562 | |
563 | |         if governance.MAX_TURNOVER <= 0:
564 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError("MAX_TURNOVER must be positive.")
565 | |
566 | |     def _validate_database(self) -> None:
567 | |
568 | |         database = self.get_database()
569 | |
570 | |         if not database.DATABASE_NAME:
571 | |             raise ConfigurationValidationError("Database name cannot be empty.")

272 | |         """

275 | |             return 0.0

284 | |         """
285 | |         Fill percentage.
286 | |         """

288 | |         if ordered_quantity == 0:
289 | |             return 0.0

296 | |         """
297 | |         Remaining quantity.
298 | |         """
299 | |

315 | |

318 | |                 "columns": cols,

321 | |                 ),
322 | |             }
323 | |

405 | |         """

412 | |     def send_template(

415 | |         """
416 | |         Send notification using template.
417 | |         """
418 | |
419 | |         if not hasattr(self, "_templates") or template not in self._templates:
420 | |             raise NotificationError(f"Unknown template '{template}'.")

422 | |         data = self._templates[template]
423 | |
424 | |         subject = data["subject"].format(**kwargs)

429 | |             channel=channel, recipient=recipient, subject=subject, message=message
430 | |         )
431 | |
432 | |         return self.send(notification)

439 | |         self,
440 | |         channel: NotificationChannel,
441 | |         recipients: list[str],
442 | |         subject: str,
443 | |         message: str,
444 | |     ) -> list[Notification]:
445 | |         """
446 | |         Broadcast notification.
447 | |         """
448 | |
449 | |         notifications = []

459 | |     # Queue Processing

463 | |         """
464 | |         Process pending notifications.
465 | |         """
466 | |
467 | |         processed = 0
468 | |
469 | |         for notification in self.pending():
470 | |             self.send(notification)
471 | |
472 | |             processed += 1
473 | |
474 | |         return processed

240 | |         order.status = OrderStatus.ROUTED
241 | |
242 | |     # =====================================================
243 | |     # Amendment
244 | |     # =====================================================
245 | |
246 | |     def amend(self, order_id: str, **updates) -> None:
247 | |         """
248 | |         Amend order.
249 | |         """
250 | |
251 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
252 | |
253 | |         if order.status in (OrderStatus.FILLED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED):
254 | |             raise OrderError("Cannot amend completed order.")
255 | |
256 | |         for key, value in updates.items():
257 | |             if hasattr(order, key):
258 | |                 setattr(order, key, value)
259 | |
260 | |     # =====================================================
261 | |     # Cancellation
262 | |     # =====================================================
263 | |
264 | |     def cancel(self, order_id: str) -> None:
265 | |         """
266 | |         Cancel order.
267 | |         """
268 | |
269 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
270 | |
271 | |         if order.status == OrderStatus.FILLED:
272 | |             raise OrderError("Filled order cannot be cancelled.")
273 | |
274 | |         order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELLED
275 | |
276 | |     # =====================================================
277 | |     # Fill Processing
278 | |     # =====================================================
279 | |
280 | |     def record_fill(self, order_id: str, quantity: float, price: float) -> None:
281 | |         """
282 | |         Record execution fill.
283 | |         """
284 | |
285 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
286 | |
287 | |         previous_quantity = order.executed_quantity
288 | |
289 | |         previous_value = previous_quantity * order.average_price
290 | |
291 | |         new_value = quantity * price
292 | |
293 | |         total_quantity = previous_quantity + quantity
294 | |
295 | |         if total_quantity > order.quantity:
296 | |             raise OrderError("Fill exceeds order quantity.")
297 | |
298 | |         order.executed_quantity = total_quantity
299 | |
300 | |         if total_quantity > 0:
301 | |             order.average_price = (previous_value + new_value) / total_quantity
302 | |
303 | |         if total_quantity == order.quantity:
304 | |             order.status = OrderStatus.FILLED
305 | |
306 | |         else:
307 | |             order.status = OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FILLED
308 | |
309 | |     # =====================================================
310 | |     # Rejection
311 | |     # =====================================================
312 | |
313 | |     def reject(self, order_id: str, reason: str = "") -> None:
314 | |         """
315 | |         Reject order.
316 | |         """
317 | |
318 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
319 | |
320 | |         order.status = OrderStatus.REJECTED
321 | |
322 | |         order.metadata["rejection_reason"] = reason
323 | |
324 | |     # =====================================================
325 | |     # Quantities
326 | |     # =====================================================
327 | |
328 | |     def remaining_quantity(self, order_id: str) -> float:
329 | |         """
330 | |         Remaining quantity.
331 | |         """
332 | |
333 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
334 | |
335 | |         return order.quantity - order.executed_quantity
336 | |
337 | |     def fill_percentage(self, order_id: str) -> float:
338 | |         """
339 | |         Fill percentage.
340 | |         """
341 | |
342 | |         order = self.get(order_id)
343 | |
344 | |         if order.quantity == 0:
345 | |             return 0.0
346 | |
347 | |         return order.executed_quantity / order.quantity
348 | |
349 | |     # =====================================================
350 | |     # Statistics
351 | |     # =====================================================
352 | |
353 | |     def statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
354 | |         """
355 | |         OMS statistics.
356 | |         """
357 | |
358 | |         return {
359 | |             "orders": len(self._orders),
360 | |             "filled": sum(
361 | |                 order.status == OrderStatus.FILLED for order in self._orders.values()
362 | |             ),
363 | |             "open": sum(
364 | |                 order.status
365 | |                 in (
366 | |                     OrderStatus.NEW,
367 | |                     OrderStatus.VALIDATED,
368 | |                     OrderStatus.ROUTED,
369 | |                     OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FILLED,
370 | |                 )
371 | |                 for order in self._orders.values()
372 | |             ),
373 | |             "cancelled": sum(
374 | |                 order.status == OrderStatus.CANCELLED for order in self._orders.values()
375 | |             ),
376 | |             "rejected": sum(
377 | |                 order.status == OrderStatus.REJECTED for order in self._orders.values()
378 | |             ),
379 | |             "enabled": self._enabled,
380 | |         }
381 | |
382 | |     # =====================================================
383 | |     # Query
384 | |     # =====================================================

401 | |         """
402 | |         Orders filtered by status.
403 | |         """
404 | |
405 | |         return [order for order in self._orders.values() if order.status == status]

409 | |         Active orders.
410 | |         """
411 | |
412 | |         return (

414 | |             + self.orders_by_status(OrderStatus.VALIDATED)
415 | |             + self.orders_by_status(OrderStatus.ROUTED)
416 | |             + self.orders_by_status(OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FILLED)
417 | |         )
418 | |

238 | |         self,

240 | |         symbol: str,
241 | |         updates: dict[str, Any],
242 | |         symbol_column: str = "symbol",
243 | |     ) -> None:
244 | |         """
245 | |         Update position.
246 | |         """
247 | |
248 | |         instance = self.get(portfolio)
249 | |
250 | |         dataframe = instance.holdings
251 | |
252 | |         mask = dataframe[symbol_column] == symbol
253 | |
254 | |         if not mask.any():
255 | |             raise PortfolioError(f"{symbol} not found.")
256 | |
257 | |         for column, value in updates.items():
258 | |             dataframe.loc[mask, column] = value

265 | |         self, portfolio: str, symbol: str, symbol_column: str = "symbol"
266 | |     ) -> pd.Series:
267 | |         """
268 | |         Return a single position.
269 | |         """
270 | |
271 | |         dataframe = self.holdings(portfolio)
272 | |
273 | |         result = dataframe.loc[dataframe[symbol_column] == symbol]
274 | |
275 | |         if result.empty:
276 | |             raise PortfolioError(symbol)
277 | |
278 | |         return result.iloc[0]

292 | |             return 0.0

336 | |     def validate(self, portfolio: str, weight_column: str = "weight") -> bool:
337 | |         """
338 | |         Validate portfolio.
339 | |         """
340 | |
341 | |         dataframe = self.holdings(portfolio)
342 | |
343 | |         if dataframe.empty:
344 | |             raise PortfolioError("Portfolio is empty.")
345 | |
346 | |         if weight_column in dataframe.columns:
347 | |             total = dataframe[weight_column].sum()
348 | |
349 | |             if abs(total - 1.0) > 0.01:
350 | |                 raise PortfolioError(f"Portfolio weights sum to {total:.4f}")
351 | |
352 | |         return True

359 | |         """

361 | |         """
362 | |
363 | |         portfolios = {}
364 | |
365 | |         for name, portfolio in self._portfolios.items():
366 | |             portfolios[name] = {
367 | |                 "holdings": len(portfolio.holdings),
368 | |                 "cash": portfolio.cash,
369 | |                 "nav": portfolio.nav,
370 | |                 "benchmark": portfolio.benchmark,
371 | |             }
372 | |
373 | |         return {"portfolio_count": len(self._portfolios), "portfolios": portfolios}
374 | |
375 | |     # =====================================================

379 | |     def sector_exposure(
380 | |         self,
381 | |         portfolio: str,

383 | |         weight_column: str = "weight",

189 | |     # =====================================================
190 | |     # Portfolio Comparison
191 | |     # =====================================================
192 | |
193 | |     def compare(
194 | |         self,
195 | |         current: pd.DataFrame,
196 | |         target: pd.DataFrame,
197 | |         symbol_column: str = "symbol",
198 | |         weight_column: str = "weight",
199 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
200 | |         """
201 | |         Compare current and target portfolios.
202 | |         """
203 | |
204 | |         comparison = current.merge(
205 | |             target, on=symbol_column, how="outer", suffixes=("_current", "_target")
206 | |         )
207 | |
208 | |         comparison = comparison.fillna(0.0)
209 | |
210 | |         comparison["weight_change"] = (
211 | |             comparison[f"{weight_column}_target"]
212 | |             - comparison[f"{weight_column}_current"]
213 | |         )
214 | |
215 | |         return comparison
216 | |
217 | |     # =====================================================
218 | |     # Trade Generation
219 | |     # =====================================================
220 | |
221 | |     def generate_trades(
222 | |         self, comparison: pd.DataFrame, symbol_column: str = "symbol"
223 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
224 | |         """
225 | |         Generate rebalance trades.
226 | |         """
227 | |
228 | |         trades = comparison.copy()
229 | |
230 | |         trades["side"] = trades["weight_change"].apply(
231 | |             lambda x: "BUY" if x > 0 else ("SELL" if x < 0 else "HOLD")
232 | |         )
233 | |
234 | |         trades["trade_weight"] = trades["weight_change"].abs()
235 | |
236 | |         return trades
237 | |
238 | |     # =====================================================
239 | |     # Turnover
240 | |     # =====================================================
241 | |
242 | |     def turnover(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
243 | |         """
244 | |         Portfolio turnover.
245 | |         """
246 | |
247 | |         return float(comparison["weight_change"].abs().sum() / 2.0)
248 | |
249 | |     # =====================================================
250 | |     # Drift
251 | |     # =====================================================
252 | |
253 | |     def drift(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
254 | |         """
255 | |         Portfolio drift.
256 | |         """
257 | |
258 | |         return float(comparison["weight_change"].abs().mean())
259 | |
260 | |     # =====================================================
261 | |     # New Positions
262 | |     # =====================================================
263 | |
264 | |     def new_positions(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
265 | |         """
266 | |         Positions entering portfolio.
267 | |         """
268 | |
269 | |         return comparison.loc[
270 | |             (comparison["weight_current"] == 0) & (comparison["weight_target"] > 0)
271 | |         ]
272 | |
273 | |     # =====================================================
274 | |     # Closed Positions
275 | |     # =====================================================
276 | |
277 | |     def closed_positions(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
278 | |         """
279 | |         Positions leaving portfolio.
280 | |         """
281 | |
282 | |         return comparison.loc[
283 | |             (comparison["weight_current"] > 0) & (comparison["weight_target"] == 0)
284 | |         ]
285 | |
286 | |     # =====================================================
287 | |     # Updated Positions
288 | |     # =====================================================
289 | |
290 | |     def updated_positions(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
291 | |         """
292 | |         Existing positions with
293 | |         modified weights.
294 | |         """
295 | |
296 | |         return comparison.loc[
297 | |             (comparison["weight_current"] > 0)
298 | |             & (comparison["weight_target"] > 0)
299 | |             & (comparison["weight_change"] != 0)
300 | |         ]
301 | |
302 | |     # =====================================================
303 | |     # Validation
304 | |     # =====================================================
305 | |
306 | |     def validate(self, comparison: pd.DataFrame) -> bool:
307 | |         """
308 | |         Validate rebalance comparison.
309 | |         """
310 | |
311 | |         required = ["weight_current", "weight_target", "weight_change"]
312 | |
313 | |         missing = [column for column in required if column not in comparison.columns]
314 | |
315 | |         if missing:
316 | |             raise RebalanceError(f"Missing columns: {missing}")
317 | |
318 | |         return True

331 | |     # =====================================================
332 | |     # Transaction Cost
333 | |     # =====================================================
334 | |
335 | |     def estimate_transaction_cost(
336 | |         self,
337 | |         trades: pd.DataFrame,
338 | |         cost_bps: float = 20.0,
339 | |         trade_weight_column: str = "trade_weight",
340 | |     ) -> float:
341 | |         """
342 | |         Estimate transaction cost in portfolio weight.
343 | |         """
344 | |
345 | |         if trade_weight_column not in trades.columns:
346 | |             raise RebalanceError(f"Missing column '{trade_weight_column}'.")
347 | |
348 | |         traded_weight = float(trades[trade_weight_column].sum())
349 | |
350 | |         return traded_weight * cost_bps / 10000.0
351 | |
352 | |     # =====================================================
353 | |     # Cash Impact
354 | |     # =====================================================
355 | |
356 | |     def cash_impact(
357 | |         self,
358 | |         trades: pd.DataFrame,
359 | |         portfolio_nav: float,
360 | |         trade_weight_column: str = "trade_weight",
361 | |         side_column: str = "side",
362 | |     ) -> dict[str, float]:
363 | |         """
364 | |         Calculate cash impact.
365 | |         """
366 | |
367 | |         buys = trades.loc[trades[side_column] == "BUY", trade_weight_column].sum()
368 | |
369 | |         sells = trades.loc[trades[side_column] == "SELL", trade_weight_column].sum()
370 | |
371 | |         return {
372 | |             "buy_value": float(buys * portfolio_nav),
373 | |             "sell_value": float(sells * portfolio_nav),
374 | |             "net_cash": float((sells - buys) * portfolio_nav),
375 | |         }
376 | |
377 | |     # =====================================================
378 | |     # Trade Summary
379 | |     # =====================================================
380 | |
381 | |     def summary(self, trades: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, Any]:
382 | |         """
383 | |         Trade summary.
384 | |         """
385 | |
386 | |         buy_count = int((trades["side"] == "BUY").sum())
387 | |
388 | |         sell_count = int((trades["side"] == "SELL").sum())
389 | |
390 | |         hold_count = int((trades["side"] == "HOLD").sum())
391 | |
392 | |         return {
393 | |             "total_trades": len(trades),
394 | |             "buy_orders": buy_count,
395 | |             "sell_orders": sell_count,
396 | |             "hold_positions": hold_count,
397 | |             "turnover": self.turnover(trades),
398 | |         }

228 | |
229 | |     def breached_limits(self, profile: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
230 | |         """
231 | |         Return breached limits.
232 | |         """
233 | |
234 | |         instance = self.get(profile)
235 | |
236 | |         breaches = {}
237 | |
238 | |         for metric, value in instance.metrics.items():
239 | |             limit = instance.limits.get(metric)
240 | |
241 | |             if limit is None:
242 | |                 continue
243 | |
244 | |             if value > limit:
245 | |                 breaches[metric] = {"value": value, "limit": limit}
246 | |
247 | |         return breaches
248 | |

321 | |
322 | |         if not instance.portfolio:

324 | |
325 | |         return True

335 | |
336 | |         breached = sum(len(self.breached_limits(profile)) for profile in self._profiles)

345 | |     # Exposure

347 | |
348 | |     def portfolio_exposure(
349 | |         self, holdings: pd.DataFrame, weight_column: str = "weight"
350 | |     ) -> float:
351 | |         """
352 | |         Calculate gross portfolio exposure.
353 | |         """
354 | |
355 | |         if weight_column not in holdings.columns:
356 | |             raise RiskError(f"Missing column '{weight_column}'.")
357 | |
358 | |         return float(holdings[weight_column].abs().sum())
359 | |

362 | |     def net_exposure(

367 | |         """
368 | |

378 | |     ) -> float:
379 | |
380 | |         return float(holdings.loc[holdings[weight_column] > 0, weight_column].sum())

401 | |         """

241 | |     # =====================================================
242 | |     # Normalization
243 | |     # =====================================================
244 | |
245 | |     def zscore(
246 | |         self, signal: str, score_column: str, output_column: str = "zscore"
247 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
248 | |         """
249 | |         Z-score normalization.
250 | |         """
251 | |
252 | |         dataframe = self.get_signal(signal).copy()
253 | |
254 | |         mean = dataframe[score_column].mean()
255 | |
256 | |         std = dataframe[score_column].std()
257 | |
258 | |         if std == 0:
259 | |             dataframe[output_column] = 0.0
260 | |
261 | |         else:
262 | |             dataframe[output_column] = (dataframe[score_column] - mean) / std
263 | |
264 | |         return dataframe

290 | |         weights: dict[str, float],
291 | |         output_column: str = "composite_score",
292 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
293 | |         """
294 | |         Weighted composite score.
295 | |         """
296 | |
297 | |         result = dataframe.copy()
298 | |
299 | |         result[output_column] = 0.0
300 | |
301 | |         total_weight = sum(weights.values())
302 | |
303 | |         if total_weight <= 0:
304 | |             raise SignalError("Weights must sum to a positive value.")
305 | |
306 | |         for column, weight in weights.items():
307 | |             if column not in result.columns:
308 | |                 raise SignalError(f"Missing column '{column}'.")
309 | |
310 | |             result[output_column] += result[column] * weight
311 | |
312 | |         result[output_column] /= total_weight
313 | |
314 | |         return result
315 | |
316 | |     # =====================================================
317 | |     # Validation
318 | |     # =====================================================
319 | |
320 | |     def validate(self, signal: str, required_columns: list[str]) -> bool:
321 | |         """
322 | |         Validate signal schema.
323 | |         """
324 | |
325 | |         dataframe = self.get_signal(signal)
326 | |
327 | |         missing = [
328 | |             column for column in required_columns if column not in dataframe.columns
329 | |         ]
330 | |
331 | |         if missing:
332 | |             raise SignalError(f"Missing columns: {missing}")
333 | |
334 | |         if dataframe.empty:
335 | |             raise SignalError("Signal dataset is empty.")
336 | |
337 | |         return True
338 | |
339 | |     # =====================================================
340 | |     # Statistics
341 | |     # =====================================================
342 | |
343 | |     def statistics(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
344 | |         """
345 | |         Signal statistics.
346 | |         """
347 | |
348 | |         return {
349 | |             "signals": len(self._signals),
350 | |             "rows": sum(len(df) for df in self._signals.values()),
351 | |             "registered": sorted(self._signals.keys()),
352 | |         }
353 | |
354 | |     # =====================================================
355 | |     # Sector Neutral Ranking
356 | |     # =====================================================
357 | |
358 | |     def sector_neutral_rank(
359 | |         self,
360 | |         signal: str,
361 | |         sector_column: str,
362 | |         score_column: str,
363 | |         output_column: str = "sector_rank",
364 | |         ascending: bool = False,
365 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
366 | |         """
367 | |         Rank securities within each sector.
368 | |         """
369 | |
370 | |         dataframe = self.get_signal(signal).copy()
371 | |
372 | |         self.validate(signal, [sector_column, score_column])
373 | |
374 | |         dataframe[output_column] = (
375 | |             dataframe.groupby(sector_column)[score_column]
376 | |             .rank(ascending=ascending, method="dense")
377 | |             .astype(int)
378 | |         )
379 | |
380 | |         return dataframe
381 | |
382 | |     # =====================================================
383 | |     # Winsorization
384 | |     # =====================================================
385 | |
386 | |     def winsorize(
387 | |         self, signal: str, column: str, lower: float = 0.01, upper: float = 0.99
388 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
389 | |         """
390 | |         Winsorize extreme values.
391 | |         """
392 | |
393 | |         dataframe = self.get_signal(signal).copy()
394 | |
395 | |         self.validate(signal, [column])
396 | |
397 | |         lower_bound = dataframe[column].quantile(lower)
398 | |
399 | |         upper_bound = dataframe[column].quantile(upper)
400 | |
401 | |         dataframe[column] = dataframe[column].clip(lower_bound, upper_bound)
402 | |
403 | |         return dataframe
404 | |
405 | |     # =====================================================
406 | |     # Standardization
407 | |     # =====================================================
408 | |
409 | |     def standardize(
410 | |         self, signal: str, column: str, output_column: str = "standardized"
411 | |     ) -> pd.DataFrame:
412 | |         """
413 | |         Min-Max normalization.
414 | |         """
415 | |
416 | |         dataframe = self.get_signal(signal).copy()
417 | |
418 | |         minimum = dataframe[column].min()
419 | |
420 | |         maximum = dataframe[column].max()
421 | |
422 | |         if minimum == maximum:
423 | |             dataframe[output_column] = 0.0
424 | |
425 | |         else:
426 | |             dataframe[output_column] = (dataframe[column] - minimum) / (
427 | |                 maximum - minimum
428 | |             )
429 | |
430 | |         return dataframe
431 | |
432 | |     # =====================================================

500 | |     def percentile(self, name: str, percentile: float) -> float:
501 | |         """
502 | |         Compute percentile.
503 | |         """
504 | |
505 | |         histogram = self._histograms.get(name)
506 | |
507 | |         if histogram is None:
508 | |             raise MetricNotFoundError(name)
509 | |
510 | |         if not histogram.values:
511 | |             return 0.0

513 | |         values = sorted(histogram.values)
514 | |
515 | |         index = int((len(values) - 1) * percentile)
516 | |

536 | |     # Timing Context
537 | |     # =====================================================
538 | |
539 | |     class TimerContext:
540 | |         def __init__(self, telemetry, metric_name: str):
541 | |
542 | |             self.telemetry = telemetry
543 | |
544 | |             self.metric_name = metric_name
545 | |
546 | |             self.started = None
547 | |
548 | |         def __enter__(self):
549 | |
550 | |             self.started = time.perf_counter()
551 | |
552 | |             return self
553 | |
554 | |         def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
555 | |
556 | |             elapsed = time.perf_counter() - self.started
557 | |
558 | |             self.telemetry.observe(self.metric_name, elapsed)
559 | |
560 | |     def timer(self, metric_name: str):
561 | |         """
562 | |         Usage
563 | |
564 | |         with telemetry.timer("optimizer"):
565 | |             ...
566 | |         """
567 | |
568 | |         return self.TimerContext(self, metric_name)

574 | |     def trace(self, operation: str, duration: float, success: bool = True) -> None:

607 | |         """

613 | |             "gauges": {key: metric.value for key, metric in self._gauges.items()},
614 | |             "histograms": {
615 | |                 key: {

617 | |                     "min": min(metric.values) if metric.values else 0,

619 | |                     "mean": (

621 | |                         if metric.values
622 | |                         else 0
623 | |                     ),
624 | |                     "p95": self.p95(key) if metric.values else 0,
625 | |                 }
626 | |                 for key, metric in self._histograms.items()
627 | |             },
628 | |             "metadata": self.metadata(),
629 | |         }

636 | |         """

686 | |

694 | |         # -------------------------------
695 | |
696 | |         for metric in self._gauges.values():
697 | |             lines.append(f"# TYPE {metric.name} gauge")
698 | |
699 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name} {metric.value}")
700 | |
701 | |         # -------------------------------
702 | |         # Histograms (summary)
703 | |         # -------------------------------
704 | |
705 | |         for metric in self._histograms.values():
706 | |             if not metric.values:
707 | |                 continue
708 | |
709 | |             lines.append(f"# TYPE {metric.name} summary")
710 | |
711 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name}_count {len(metric.values)}")
712 | |
713 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name}_sum {sum(metric.values)}")
714 | |
715 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name}_p50 {self.p50(metric.name)}")
716 | |
717 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name}_p95 {self.p95(metric.name)}")
718 | |
719 | |             lines.append(f"{metric.name}_p99 {self.p99(metric.name)}")
720 | |
721 | |         return "\n".join(lines)
